<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>End</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {
	display: flex;
	gap: 46px;
}

.column {
	min-width: 0;
	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {
	display: block;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	line-height: 1.3;
	padding: 0.125rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {
	margin-left: 1.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-2 {
	margin-left: 3rem;
}

.table_of_contents-indent-3 {
	margin-left: 4.5rem;
}

.table_of_contents-link {
	text-decoration: none;
	opacity: 0.7;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.18);
}

table,
th,
td {
	border: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
	border-collapse: collapse;
}

table {
	border-left: none;
	border-right: none;
}

th,
td {
	font-weight: normal;
	padding: 0.25em 0.5em;
	line-height: 1.5;
	min-height: 1.5em;
	text-align: left;
}

th {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
}

ol,
ul {
	margin: 0;
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
	margin-block-end: 0.6em;
}

li > ol:first-child,
li > ul:first-child {
	margin-block-start: 0.6em;
}

ul > li {
	list-style: disc;
}

ul.to-do-list {
	padding-inline-start: 0;
}

ul.to-do-list > li {
	list-style: none;
}

.to-do-children-checked {
	text-decoration: line-through;
	opacity: 0.375;
}

ul.toggle > li {
	list-style: none;
}

ul {
	padding-inline-start: 1.7em;
}

ul > li {
	padding-left: 0.1em;
}

ol {
	padding-inline-start: 1.6em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-2 {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

ol.numbered-list.numbered-list-digits-3plus {
	padding-inline-start: 2.4em;
}

ol > li {
	padding-left: 0.2em;
}

.mono ol {
	padding-inline-start: 2em;
}

.mono ol > li {
	text-indent: -0.4em;
}

.toggle {
	padding-inline-start: 0em;
	list-style-type: none;
}

/* Indent toggle children */
.toggle > li > details {
	padding-left: 1.7em;
}

.toggle > li > details > summary {
	margin-left: -1.1em;
}

.selected-value {
	display: inline-block;
	padding: 0 0.5em;
	background: rgba(206, 205, 202, 0.5);
	border-radius: 3px;
	margin-right: 0.5em;
	margin-top: 0.3em;
	margin-bottom: 0.3em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.collection-title {
	display: inline-block;
	margin-right: 1em;
}

.page-description {
	margin-bottom: 2em;
}

.simple-table {
	margin-top: 1em;
	font-size: 0.875rem;
	empty-cells: show;
}
.simple-table td {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table th {
	height: 29px;
	min-width: 120px;
}

.simple-table-header-color {
	background: rgb(247, 246, 243);
	color: black;
}
.simple-table-header {
	font-weight: 500;
}

time {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

.icon {
	display: inline-flex;
	align-items: center;
	justify-content: center;
	max-width: 1.2em;
	max-height: 1.2em;
	text-decoration: none;
	vertical-align: text-bottom;

}

img.icon {

}

.callout img.notion-static-icon {

	height: 1em;
}

}

.callout h2,
.callout h3 {

}

.user-icon {

}

.user-icon-inner {
	font-size: 0.8em;
}

.text-icon {
	border: 1px solid #000;
	text-align: center;
}

.page-cover-image {
	display: block;
	object-fit: cover;
	width: 100%;
	max-height: 30vh;
}

.page-header-icon {
	font-size: 3rem;
	margin-bottom: 1rem;
}

.page-header-icon-with-cover {
	margin-top: -0.72em;
	margin-left: 0.07em;
}

.page-header-icon img {
	border-radius: 3px;
}

.link-to-page {
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border: none;
	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {
	white-space: nowrap;
}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;
	padding: 0;
	border-radius: 0;
	text-align: center;
}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;
	border-radius: 3px;
	font-size: 85%;
	tab-size: 2;
}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;
	color: inherit;
}

blockquote {
	font-size: 1em;
	margin: 1em 0;
	padding-left: 1em;
	border-left: 3px solid rgb(55, 53, 47);
}

blockquote.quote-large {
	font-size: 1.25em;
}

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;
	overflow: hidden;
	text-overflow: ellipsis;
	height: 1.75em;
	white-space: nowrap;
}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;
	flex: 1 1 180px;
	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {
	color: rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.6);
	font-size: 0.75em;
	overflow: hidden;
	max-height: 4.5em;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-b77e-ffb4a5f60fa5" class="">End</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-b915-ed7ebdfd271b" class="">Gap-closure: Grand Unified Loop Kernel (complete, executable spec)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-9bd1-ff776bd7dfd9" class="">This closes the missing pieces by making every term explicit: <strong>state vector, update equations, gates, regime logic, “intangible” handling, ownership, cross-species coupling, outputs, and a deterministic scenario DSL</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b0ac-c7a5ab7dc4c7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-bc72-f3d64e751373" class="">1) Canonical state (single source of truth)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-816b-c719c7b88888" class="">1.1 Global state vector</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-9041-fc2b7746516b" class="">At tick , the integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" x_t=
\Big[
q_t,\ G_t,\ U_t,\ \Xi_t,\ R_t,\ D_t,\ P_t,\ M_t,\ T_t,\ H_t,\ \Pi_t,\ \Theta_t
\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-934f-ea5979195e03" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-877c-e01ff8c922ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= constraint density (initial boundary constraint budget still “holding”)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-a6ce-c0c399e9775f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= usable gradient / free-energy slope available for work</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-a1a6-fa88e150248b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= unwritten environment capacity (write-once budget remaining)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-ac2b-c9b63b58a3a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= effective noise/overwrite intensity</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-92be-cc4f7818464e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= stable record mass (redundant, error-corrected traces)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b798-c46fe20c5fa6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= recursion depth (stacked self-model control depth)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-b44e-e39fccfbffb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= available power for compute+repair</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-ad4a-d4f087f3643c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= accessible memory budget (bits or normalized units)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-949c-f9ebb0f6168d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= temperature proxy (for Landauer coupling)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-83ca-fecbc037cb94" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= horizon/closure proxy (caps memory and write budget)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b805-fbe170032d1e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= permission/ownership matrix (access control)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-834f-fff779b5654b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= parameter set (all constants; deterministic)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-b94f-fa75b1a54884" class="">All variables are <strong>non-negative scalars</strong> except  (matrix) and  (struct).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-8672-ee24e4dcd041" class="">1.2 Cross-species extension (required)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-a320-fb470052e263" class="">For  species/agents indexed by :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-b8bc-d01eecd5cdf2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t=
\Big[
q_t,\ G_t,\ U_t,\ \Xi_t,\ R_t,\
\{D_{i,t},P_{i,t},M_{i,t},E_{i,t}\}_{i=1}^S,\
T_t,H_t,\Pi_t,\Theta_t
\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-af8a-c3dc0c5f223b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= agent internal error (control error accumulator)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-8aa5-eaa78f24beeb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Each agent has its own depth/power/memory/error, but shares environment .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-884a-e77e9b23bbf1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b90a-d0f6bc7f8ebe" class="">2) Deterministic update law</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8494-c6d82ff8a26e" class="">All updates below are <strong>pure functions</strong>. No randomness. If “stochastic” is needed, it is generated by a deterministic pseudo-stream seeded from  (still deterministic).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-ba42-cb7a6f474dc5" class="">2.1 Constraint unwind (Birth→Expansion driver)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-9f94-d5f99775bfc9" class="">Constraint density monotonically decreases (unwinds) unless externally reinforced:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-a04c-c911658f369b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1}=\max\{0,\ q_t - \lambda_q \cdot \phi_q(G_t,\Xi_t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-a7b7-cb0c0d96924b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_q(G,\Xi)=\frac{G}{1+\Xi}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8df6-e7cd5b037d9f" class="">Interpretation: constraints unwind faster when gradients are usable and noise is low.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-ae44-da5addcffe2a" class="">2.2 Gradient dynamics (resource slope)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-8f6d-ec3cfcd751fa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=\max\{0,\ G_t + \lambda_G \cdot \psi(G_t,q_t) - \mu_G \cdot \chi(R_t,\Xi_t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-a3f2-e16f46c8c7be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi(G,q)=q - \rho_G G \quad;\quad \chi(R,\Xi)=\Xi R</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-95f4-cedcfa9bcb32" class="">Interpretation: early high  enables long-lived gradients; high noise + record maintenance drains gradient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-b798-c309605bfaf9" class="">2.3 Write-capacity (environment “fresh DOF”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-9186-f6e3692b2b74" class="">Records consume write-capacity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-9dc7-f23a50606511" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=\max\{0,\ U_t - \gamma_U \cdot \Delta R_t^{+}\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-b427-e7602b39575b" class="">This is <strong>write-once</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9c06-eeb6f7b27d98" class="">2.4 Noise / overwrite dynamics</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-aa01-f56dc7114165" class="">Noise rises with contention, decay, and adversarial overwrite:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-9671-ce860a4e233f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{t+1}=\max\{0,\ \Xi_t + \lambda_{\Xi}\cdot \upsilon(t,\text{regime}) - \mu_{\Xi}\cdot \omega(G_t)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9142-fdda2bf962ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\omega(G)=\frac{G}{1+G}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-a2ad-de4ca6a98936"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-a7d4-edcf65c76087" class="">3) Records as coded traces (no gaps)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9a4c-c9c5b0cd28ae" class="">3.1 Code parameters (explicit)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-af8d-d04925a6f172" class="">Let redundancy factor  and code threshold function  be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-9667-e2d65153c42c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = 1 + \eta_r \cdot \frac{G_t}{1+\Xi_t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b3b5-e9ac74dd01cf" class="">p_{\text{th}}(r)=1-\exp(-\kappa_r (r-1))<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-a20b-d9ed3e5fcd71" class="">Noise-to-failure probability proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-9a6d-ec5fa26fc0d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(\Xi_t)=1-\exp(-\kappa_{\Xi}\Xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-ad09-db64a11d2595" class="">3.2 Record update (with catastrophic threshold)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9eab-d857f86e173d" class="">Define Arrow productivity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-bbf5-e9f8bf4bea78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R^{\text{build}}_t=\beta_R \cdot G_t \cdot \mathbb{1}[U_t&gt;0]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-bb83-cc99a48e8990" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R^{\text{erosion}}_t=\kappa_R \cdot \Xi_t \cdot R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-a1bb-e254be40e1e7" class="">Catastrophic code failure indicator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-8840-f172c4d5a313" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{CodeFail}_t=\mathbb{1}\big[p(\Xi_t)\ge p_{\text{th}}(r_t)\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-9544-fa59a5a7a20e" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-a6ac-d33ec189693f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=
\max\left\{
0,\
R_t
+\Delta R^{\text{build}}_t
-\Delta R^{\text{erosion}}_t
-\lambda_R \cdot \text{CodeFail}_t \cdot R_t
\right\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-8a99-c31f72a22b27" class="">This fully specifies “records” without handwaving.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-9808-ea368d8c010f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-87af-f2c0143c2d86" class="">4) Recursion depth (control + delay + energy + memory)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-866b-c4d07a4089dc" class="">For each agent :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-8509-eaa263d6ac7c" class="">4.1 Control error dynamics with delay</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9801-e51399996721" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{i,t+1} =
\alpha_i(D_{i,t})E_{i,t}
+\eta_i(\Xi_t)
-\rho_i \cdot u_{i,t-\tau_i}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-90df-c648474d4fed" class="">Minimal choices:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-80cb-f349626d938a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i(D)=1+\kappa_D \cdot D
\quad;\quad
\eta_i(\Xi)=\kappa_\eta \Xi
\quad;\quad
u_{i,t}=\frac{P_{i,t}}{1+P_{i,t}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-899f-fb7424ed2d48" class="">Delay  grows with depth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9b26-d4f56617e53d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_i = \tau_0 + \tau_D \cdot D_{i,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8059-b3ca-e060605a2500" class="">4.2 Landauer power requirement</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-8d78-c1bbc7643b5c" class="">Let required erase rate scale:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-a4ad-eb2c1ad6a395" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot B(D)=b_0 + b_1 D + b_2 D^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-a82d-e6ace3af2443" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{\min}_{i,t}=kT_t\ln 2 \cdot \dot B(D_{i,t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-8a37-e20fa2e049ec" class="">4.3 Memory requirement</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-9585-fe0aff611299" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^{\text{models}}_{i,t}=m_0 + m_1 D_{i,t} + m_2 D_{i,t}^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-84ad-f543caf0c7af" class="">I^{\text{records}}_t = c_R \cdot R_t<br/><br/>Total:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-a8e5-dd1b4edf47be" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^{\text{total}}_{i,t}=I^{\text{models}}_{i,t}+I^{\text{records}}_t
\le M_{i,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-973b-f4f5fc965991" class="">4.4 Depth update rule (explicit)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-857c-d4f190fa8fbe" class="">Depth can increase only if all gates pass:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-b9de-c4bdb94cc878" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{i,t+1}=
\begin{cases}
D_{i,t}+1 &amp; \text{if } \text{DepthGate}_{i,t}=1 \\
\max(0,D_{i,t}-1) &amp; \text{if } \text{DepthCollapse}_{i,t}=1 \\
D_{i,t} &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-ac22-f75ad9b39b11" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-a1de-ce0271192c65" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>DepthGate</strong> requires:<div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-86e6-e6b1aeab9962" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-baba-d3d2d12971f5" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a482-eacdefb5458f" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Control stability:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-b842-f0540b7d4fd0" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Environment:</li></ol></div></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-9541-fd84cd181c86" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>DepthCollapse</strong> triggers if any of (1–3) fails hard for consecutive ticks (deterministic counter).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-b790-d6bfb66f2493" class="">This closes the “recursion depth” gap: it is now executable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-aa33-f024120171ff"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-be82-fd054d97104e" class="">5) Ownership + “all information has an owner” (formal)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9d0d-ecc4c7710f45" class="">5.1 Permission matrix</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-9e26-e53caf9a4a13" class="">Let  be access probabilities/permissions (deterministic, not random), interpreted as allowed bandwidth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-b9ae-ecd9f7e727dc" class="">A read/write attempt from node  with demand  is allowed iff:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-bc8d-c36f2a4bfd01" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
w_{ab,t} \le \Pi_{ab,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-85d5-fbeceb9a93a1" class="">Failing this is a <strong>hard invariant breach</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-b417-d2c6918f38a3" class="">5.2 Ownership influence on records</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-bd53-d3483f43966c" class="">Records can only be formed from accessible channels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-9a39-f2170941b4c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R^{\text{build}}_t
=\beta_R G_t \cdot \mathbb{1}[U_t&gt;0]\cdot \mathbb{1}[\text{AccessOK}_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-87d3-c272f28b8a78"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-8068-f46e431fefbc" class="">6) “Intangible” channels (WiFi/telepathy/etc.) without ungrounded claims</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-aeb2-de9686769f8e" class="">They are modeled as <strong>candidate channels</strong>  that can contribute to prediction/coordination only if they pass gates identical in form to tangible channels.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-b0b2-f73203a6809d" class="">6.1 Channel evidence state</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-8939-ff3ccc12a78a" class="">For each channel :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-a278-c762f106885e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= prediction lift over baseline (measured in-sim)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-92b7-c3ddd35b8d24" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= false positive rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-83fc-c2d6cc4259fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= stability/repeatability score</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-a8fe-d108bc10d00c" class="">Update deterministically from scenario data + outcomes (no handwaving).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-987c-d9bb73b5a44e" class="">6.2 Channel gate</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-ac30-d0452e3a2d38" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{ChanOK}_{c,t}=
\mathbb{1}\left[
L_{c,t}\ge L_{\min}
\ \wedge\
F_{c,t}\le F_{\max}
\ \wedge\
S_{c,t}\ge S_{\min}
\ \wedge\
\Pi\ \text{permits it}
\right]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-9255-c6c6e3c8bffd" class="">6.3 Channel contribution (bounded)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-9ca4-d62b6c388f92" class="">If , allow it to reduce effective noise or increase effective gradient <strong>by a capped amount</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-9084-f8c22f6a9540" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi^{\text{eff}}_t=\Xi_t\cdot \left(1-\sum_c \delta_{\Xi,c}\text{ChanOK}_{c,t}\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-b585-f386e5595720" class="">G^{\text{eff}}<em>t=G_t\cdot \left(1+\sum_c \delta</em>{G,c}\text{ChanOK}_{c,t}\right)<br/><br/>with caps to prevent runaway:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-9ca6-c5ac65cc4aec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
0\le \sum_c \delta_{\Xi,c}\text{ChanOK}_{c,t}\le \Delta_{\Xi}^{\max}
,\quad
0\le \sum_c \delta_{G,c}\text{ChanOK}_{c,t}\le \Delta_{G}^{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802e-aadb-c91fdad02e3d" class="">This is the <strong>closed gap</strong>: “intangible” is allowed only as a gated, measurable lift inside the model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-b96c-eaad78b2e0f2"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-ae9f-eeaf5b88bf40" class="">7) Regime logic (Birth→Expansion→Dominance→Decay) as explicit classifier</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-a6c5-ec5b31b76ec6" class="">Define thresholds  in .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ada8-e4ec93cd0e58" class="">A deterministic regime function:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-942d-d9fd1c2295e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Birth (B) if and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-bf48-eab979301237" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Expansion (E) if and and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-b6ab-e906cf6f6684" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dominance (Dc) if and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-bde5-d77607fe67ac" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decay (De) if and</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-acb5-d9c91ce79905" class="">Regime must be <strong>total and exclusive</strong>: ties resolve by fixed precedence: De &gt; Dc &gt; E &gt; B.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-bab8-d7fd32ee3adf"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-a24d-e776930001dc" class="">8) Gates (final consolidated list; p(\Xi_t^{\text{eff}}) &lt; p_{\text{th}}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-a654-f9f67090fa6d" class="">Gate C ControlGate (per agent)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-9744-c885d08a62e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{i,t}\le E_{\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-a962-c363c5fafb35" class="">Gate D BudgetGate (per agent)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-89c1-e289c2ae474a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{i,t}\ge kT_t\ln2\cdot \dot B(D_{i,t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-b389-f6894e5cc25f" class="">Gate E MemoryGate (per agent)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8db6-ec06f2c8b3cb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I^{\text{total}}_{i,t}\le M_{i,t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-bc37-fc4447afbb9d" class="">Gate F WriteCapGate</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-857f-ca00594762b9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

bypass, Intangible bypass, “free records”.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-8498-dc9816b175fa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 style="list-style-type:disc"><code>scenario_id</code></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-96db-f31ded0f0a08" class="bulleted-list"><li updates</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-92f3-efb1d35fbc24" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><code>access_events[t]</code> (who tries to read/write what)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-b85a-cac5f20f423d" class="">This fully removes ambiguity: the simulator never asks
