\exists \text{ correlations } C \text{ such that } I(C) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-97ef-e4afd5bd48b6" class="">but</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-8765-ce9552681a3a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Current instrumentation sensitivity} &lt; \text{required SNR threshold}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8f5d-da42f9e9a668" class="">That does not violate physics. It simply means:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-93b5-e0c091c9c4a4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S/N &lt; \theta_{detect}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-bc5d-d1089912b049" class="">for present devices.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-a899-f126322ee8fd" class="">Science progresses by improving signal detection thresholds.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-9b5b-e3a9815c5a7a" class="">However, accessibility requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-931d-d58990ffcf31" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A physical carrier.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-9b0d-c75414346b51" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A coupling mechanism.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-adb9-ce96ef6b4e50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Measurable mutual information.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-86e0-da7db4357778" class="">Without a carrier or coupling mechanism, “accessible” becomes undefined in operational terms.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-9527-f159ee25fe81"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-8d2a-cdd471a5d7df" class="">2) Patterns recurring across civilisation and time</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-8912-c0e5aacadbb3" class="">This is a different statement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-a5f0-c4373fff4a64" class="">Patterns can persist not because specific micro-information survives intact, but because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-85c7-d0cf9456454c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Dynamics } F(x) \text{ generate attractors}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a943-f837b1418356" class="">In dynamical systems:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-891d-ca42643eb5c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1} = F(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-96c1-d7f9f5d070be" class="">If the system has attractors , then trajectories repeatedly converge toward those structures.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-b8ba-d5f2c4e1081c" class="">So recurring civilizational patterns can emerge because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-ae58-c723c75f2f7c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Human cognition has structural constraints.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-b9b0-d85690172cb6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Resource gradients impose repeated selection pressures.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-a7c0-f30439c3a50f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Social control loops follow similar stability equations.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-936c-fffa0fc74b8f" class="">This does not require micro-information persistence from past individuals.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-8c3a-d91ce7142316" class="">It requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-984e-c0d5d9291a9d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Similar constraints} \Rightarrow \text{Similar attractors}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8605-e7ff97cff835" class="">This is mathematically sufficient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a1-a2f6-e76d99ad17c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-adbe-cacfceb0e980" class="">3) “All information has an owner”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-ba0a-c9fcd37a6cba" class="">This is the strongest claim and requires definition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-8490-ca450c3b55a4" class="">Information in physics is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-8de2-d0a417f0f0f5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{A measure of correlations between variables}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-9829-f535cf387985" class="">Ownership is a social or boundary concept.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9d45-c4ce1ed5f246" class="">Ownership requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-ab09-f9bc4751a294" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A defined boundary</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9886-c64ed15bfbff" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Control rights over state transitions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8048-bcbc-c2c85b8afaeb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Enforcement capability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-b303-f94614d2d5d3" class="">Formally, ownership of information  requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-be13-f72895d4102c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists B \text{ such that } \Delta V(I | control(B)) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-ac11-fba1e8ee8cde" class="">Meaning:<br/>Only if controlling that boundary changes outcomes does ownership have operational meaning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-83a9-e773fecc2641" class="">At the cosmological level, information does not have owners.<br/>It has locations in phase space.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-8e89-ec0f417ebb58" class="">Ownership emerges only when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9323-d16b9d434bf5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agents exist.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-98be-d0ed42b37bc4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agents maintain boundaries.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-8d4f-e9769e9581d6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Agents enforce constraints.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-a73a-c73eed8b6231" class="">So ownership is an emergent property of recursive boundary systems,<br/>not a fundamental property of information itself.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-9a21-e0525f76fe0d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8cd0-db6eaa6e5cf2" class="">4) Spiritual pattern mapping</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-96ce-fdf8f9a14b7e" class="">When people speak of “spiritual patterns,” this often corresponds structurally to:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-bf6f-f67815222d2d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Recognition of attractors.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-8479-c46100566d8e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Recognition of invariant structures across scales.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-9ab7-c7c6b756508c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Recognition of recurrent loops under constraint.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-b62b-c3d2d35c5db7" class="">That does not require violation of physics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-9e77-fea66b63cd03" class="">It requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-96ea-e3202af96755" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Cross-scale invariance detection}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-a639-c406894cd6ed" class="">which is a cognitive function.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-b48b-cd8d9a93b129" class="">It becomes problematic only if it asserts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-be7b-e9d820d33dd2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Non-local signaling without carrier.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-9f6c-d6da57b35348" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Violation of conservation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8d4d-cd303b0bcc2f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Superluminal transfer.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-918b-fa87dd2b3be7" class="">Those would require new physics and empirical evidence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-a23a-c67bebd6d17b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-b491-d5f72201c0e6" class="">5) The critical distinction</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8e1f-d2f262027f78" class="">There are three layers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-adc3-f027588a0955" class="">Layer 1:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b4b2-d9be13bb0ae1" class="">Energy and micro-information persist under known laws.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-afa8-d4bcbbf544c9" class="">Layer 2:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-ab7a-fb0da6b62c2f" class="">Macro-patterns recur because dynamics + constraints produce attractors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-822a-f78d10cec8f9" class="">Layer 3:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b57e-cdb168bd3bd3" class="">Ownership and meaning are emergent properties of bounded recursive agents.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-99bc-ceaf54c4fee8" class="">Conflating them creates conceptual confusion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-afc8-deb9ed400faa"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-a2c3-cce3ba6910a4" class="">6) What remains logically open</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8047-8936-e6c5e1b0527a" class="">Three possibilities remain open without contradiction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8108-cd2b55dabcbc" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>There may be weak physical channels not yet well-characterized.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-82d2-df74ead5b541" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>There may be large-scale attractor dynamics that mimic continuity across time.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-ab90-c5ae56ecd193" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Subjective experience may correspond to internal recursive modeling of large-scale patterns.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-9c33-e19bdf7244d4" class="">What is not established:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-a212-e5a4a2cb67f1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Demonstrated non-physical information channels.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-91a2-ee40fcf016a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Empirical violation of locality or conservation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-8687-fbbd1b17f768"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-a585-d43f08a2f4d7" class="">If you want to go deeper, the next real frontier is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b0d4-f367a8fbbbd1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Whether “meaning” can be formalized as a conserved structural invariant across agents.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-b71f-f8d1456ef7cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Whether consciousness corresponds to a specific class of recursive attractor in high-dimensional state space.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-80b1-ef3b7ee560c3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Whether cross-agent pattern resonance can be modeled without violating physics.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-8751-ecc5d41e64f6" class="">Say one direction and we will formalize it.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-be4f-c7e43fd63fe3" class="">We need to separate what is physically measurable from what is metaphorical.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-9e55-d93853fed09d" class="">There are three different claims hidden inside your sentence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-bc96-df255e0308e2" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Energy and information persist in the environment.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-95d0-ef91ddc09b09" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Machines can access environmental energy/information (WiFi).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a4e2-c8789df44f25" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Telepathy implies non-local access to information without known physical carriers.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a53e-e345db0336e5" class="">Each must be treated differently.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-8eb2-cf4fab2c3f4b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-a836-ff21cfd3c0b8" class="">1) WiFi is ordinary electromagnetic propagation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-ac1a-de8f9eec8fd4" class="">WiFi works because:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a331-f40fb6e8cb09" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Electromagnetic fields satisfy Maxwell’s equations.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-a20e-f2de2d371e44" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Antennas couple to EM fields.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-ae4e-d6c55147c76b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Signals propagate at finite speed .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-ad8e-f612d5add602" class="">Maxwell’s equations (vacuum form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-abf9-d55782810726" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla \cdot \mathbf{E} = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-87c4-d2f08f7a1d53" class="">\nabla \cdot \mathbf{B} = 0<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a588-e282aa7ae890" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla \times \mathbf{E} = -\frac{\partial \mathbf{B}}{\partial t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9084-ee4d1a839034" class="">\nabla \times \mathbf{B} = \mu_0\epsilon_0 \frac{\partial \mathbf{E}}{\partial t}<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-9aa3-c15eafeda377" class="">A transmitter encodes bits into modulated EM waves:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-bac6-eb17f54474ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E(t) = A(t)\cos(\omega t + \phi(t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-a556-d1ca49113ed2" class="">A receiver extracts:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-a37e-d0f24706b534" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{b}_t = \text{decode}(E(t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-a314-e379c7622220" class="">So WiFi is not mysterious persistence of information.<br/>It is controlled modulation of an EM field within a finite bandwidth and signal-to-noise ratio.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-b0bd-c1bb7b50e737" class="">Shannon capacity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-8e33-e9fca0e132b3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C = B \log_2(1 + \frac{S}{N})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-b952-f0cec5ee11eb" class="">If signal-to-noise ratio drops below threshold, information cannot be recovered.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9039-e35e7ed13f8c" class="">So accessibility requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-90c1-e901af357499" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A carrier field.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-97c5-d3e3d313682b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Modulation.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-9906-c7ffc2081844" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Sufficient SNR.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-92de-e67775725d29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">A compatible receiver.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-8623-f4e8686a33ae"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-a444-fa2301fa9a7a" class="">2) Energy existing ≠ information accessible</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-8913-f388fb60801a" class="">Energy may exist everywhere, but structured information requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-bc8b-e5016d64925b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{mutual information} &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-a75c-dc4ac3afd00c" class="">Between transmitter  and receiver :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-87db-c34c11dec15a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(T:R) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-8fd3-e5164c746b58" class="">If correlations are diluted into thermal noise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-a940-d3ec19e06ada" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(T:R) \to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-a2b2-efd9f17a13ff" class="">Then no machine can reconstruct structure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9596-c55974f2df04" class="">After biological death, correlations are dispersed into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a395-dfc9316361d4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">thermal motion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-8af2-e61930dc9726" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">chemical diffusion</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a0d4-c93c7af85e6e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">radiation</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a815-e29ccbcfb630" class="">The environment retains microscopic correlations in principle,<br/>but the mutual information between those microstates and the prior organized system becomes effectively zero for any realistic receiver.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-8ff7-f49159772dde"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bb11-dd5d56ee5928" class="">3) Telepathy requires a physical channel</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-920a-feb437887f63" class="">For information transfer, one of the following must hold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-8e25-d919bdeb9b0f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Classical signal via known field (EM, acoustic, chemical)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-8149-c569baa8dae2" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Quantum channel (entanglement-assisted but not superluminal)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-bb3b-e18f88ab3362" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>New undiscovered interaction</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-8868-dd335796ed88" class="">Quantum mechanics does not allow faster-than-light signaling via entanglement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-ba73-e3f636cf6a43" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(a|x,y) = p(a|x)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-a00c-e8aa787f5c51" class="">No-signaling theorem.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-84d7-ddc9eec320f3" class="">If telepathy exists in physical terms, it must satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-9c3e-d8a51f086b27" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(A:B) &gt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8090-920a-ed14a610082e" class="">and obey locality constraints.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-a100-d4eca30712af" class="">Currently, no reproducible experimental evidence demonstrates a non-EM, non-sensory information channel between brains.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-9c74-db19b28913bb" class="">So within known physics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-afae-ff92e0a06027" class="">Telepathy without a carrier violates either:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-a809-f7a899d0dea1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">locality</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-96a0-f26d20c94478" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">conservation laws</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-96fc-f6293fa7f58b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">no-signaling constraints</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-9119-dfed8cefc9b7" class="">None have been experimentally overturned.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-b744-fa00b26d6c84"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-b5de-f24fe41e48d9" class="">4) Could machines access distributed environmental information?</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-94c9-ef540987f6f0" class="">Yes — but only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-a0ec-c948c18b642d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>The information is encoded in a physical field.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-9194-de940ab82b3d" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>The field retains coherent structure.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-a3c3-c383e55c67f2" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>The receiver is sensitive enough.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-a704-d3a7a16de720" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>Noise does not dominate.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-aad4-e28765ce3d60" class="">For example:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-8e44-eb41d2036ff2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Radio telescopes detect cosmic microwave background.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-94cc-f702440fbc91" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">LIGO detects gravitational waves.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-a4df-efe694c4decf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">MRI detects nuclear spin alignment.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a473-d86e2528c4b6" class="">These are physical, field-mediated interactions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-935c-fbd78623b755" class="">But retrieving fine-grained information about a past organism from environmental noise would require:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-9bc8-c2b92dffde5c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Signal power} \gg \text{noise power}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-9cca-ce76aaad05db" class="">In practice, after diffusion:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-bdb7-db7cd7f81874" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S/N \to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8db9-e36408e0c8db" class="">Reconstruction becomes physically infeasible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8086-ad82-e2779885e5f9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-9336-fd802916ccfe" class="">5) The deeper constraint: accessibility is bandwidth-limited and thermodynamically bounded</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-8738-f72885df78fc" class="">Any receiver extracting information must expend energy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a0-9b45-d943ba5b25ec" class="">Minimum work per bit:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-80fa-e7ffbea5f1d1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{min} = kT\ln 2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-be33-fd96d38167fd" class="">If environmental correlations are spread across enormous phase space, the cost to reconstruct grows combinatorially.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-81cf-e8dc95503017" class="">So even if information is not destroyed, accessibility is bounded by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-b6a2-fc89cd82bf4e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Energy budget} \ge kT\ln 2 \cdot \text{bits extracted}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b2b6-d88e577ee14e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-8e44-e0ca93d9c63d" class="">6) So what survives and what is accessible?</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8164-c9f6d8eedcdc" class="">Energy survives — always.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a702-d739590c5db8" class="">Quantum information survives globally — in principle.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9e3a-f65a33801a6e" class="">But accessibility requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a4bf-dfa6e0e280e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{Carrier coherence} + \text{SNR} + \text{receiver coupling}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-b610-dfe7e10c795b" class="">WiFi satisfies this.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-b6eb-fdc8555cd9c4" class="">Telepathy, as currently understood, does not have demonstrated carrier or SNR above noise.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b73d-f625f415f389"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-9f99-f1ac618c529a" class="">7) The final structural distinction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-84f5-dafb99ed5a9b" class="">Persistence ≠ accessibility</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-b6fd-d476b7d2c56b" class="">Accessibility ≠ recoverability</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-aa2f-feb6c0b76c21" class="">Recoverability ≠ organized selfhood</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8717-f1825eaad06b" class="">After death:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-a610-f64918dd8960" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Energy persists.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801c-8a5b-c3bdd92b309d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Microscopic correlations persist.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-8d74-fa70b558d156" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Organized boundary-controlled self does not persist.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-b2ab-e71f8e238add" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Accessible mutual information with that structure rapidly approaches zero.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-b1e7-eb633a09b15d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-80eb-cb48324487a2" class="">If you want to go deeper, the next directions are:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-bddb-cd5845f93ec9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Quantum information retention in black holes</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-8c3a-e6e8f792ca2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Decoherence theory in biological systems</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-99b7-dc0849f75544" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM brain field strength vs environmental noise analysis</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b5ad-dce65d9f0e93" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Reconstruction limits from thermodynamic irreversibility</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-bc42-c58310ed3515" class="">Choose one word.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-be21-e8f087ce136c" class="">We can go deeper — but we must separate three different layers that often get conflated:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-aa50-e9d0e1e331f0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Conservation of physical energy</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-9d14-f43bbdfd2a63" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Persistence of physical information</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a9b4-cb2f43de8f31" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Persistence of organized structure / self-model</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-8d38-cac34d3b9c91" class="">“Energy and information exist before birth and after death” is physically true in one sense, false in another, and undefined in a third. The missing piece is <strong>which layer survives</strong> and under what formal conditions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8190-f2087820131b" class="">Below is the clean structural decomposition.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-a4f8-f0a3974b3574"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-9abb-f87fd8e7c67c" class="">1) Energy does not disappear — but structure does</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8236-fc5c5312e9c6" class="">Local conservation law:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9dda-f4981edb6e69" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\nabla_\mu T^{\mu\nu} = 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-910e-e580756f3caa" class="">Energy–momentum is conserved. When an organism dies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-9386-ff3299bb4260" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Chemical energy → heat + molecular redistribution</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-be7e-d94976c0e803" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Electric potentials → dissipate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-8b9a-ea0f29f492fb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">EM emissions → radiate outward</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-9a09-fadf3f4f00d0" class="">Energy persists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9335-f92aa16c358c" class="">But structured organization is not conserved.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-a837-dc6cf74201af" class="">Define structural organization  as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-8535-d1b8678c299b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{O}(t) = \text{mutual information among system components}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-bdd3-cacccc18ce05" class="">After death:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-bc63-f4992195abe2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{O}(t) &lt; 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-bf2f-da8fa2db4ecc" class="">Energy remains, but high-order correlations decay.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-bd7e-ca1818efd2a3" class="">So the first overlooked distinction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-a4a7-e4d00a226def" class="">Conservation ≠ preservation of organization.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-87f1-e82c58b3ce94"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-920c-fced7fac69a7" class="">2) Information is conserved in unitary physics — but not locally accessible</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9b3c-c5350ad9adfb" class="">In quantum mechanics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b1b8-dca465d19b70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
|\psi(t)\rangle = U(t)|\psi(0)\rangle</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-8cba-eff2b37f3ca3" class="">Unitary evolution preserves von Neumann entropy globally.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-81e7-f0e4d526ad1c" class="">So in principle:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-868b-c18ca3917a16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{fine}(t) = S_{fine}(0)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-85ae-d74f9ac9987d" class="">But local observers only access reduced density matrices:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-bf4e-ed13cbf682fd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_S = \mathrm{Tr}_E(|\psi\rangle\langle\psi|)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-9370-ee1aa6f91ba6" class="">Local entropy increases:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-9e24-fd4ccb22591a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S(\rho_S(t)) \ge S(\rho_S(0))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-a13c-f0f3eb2dfbec" class="">So information is not destroyed; it becomes <strong>distributed into environment correlations</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8d90-e1e5ced8e7b5" class="">Second overlooked distinction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-bf0d-f195d1739240" class="">Information persists globally, but usable information degrades locally.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8820-d13b7ad35fc1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-b1d3-f1653e2c6992" class="">3) Before birth: energy/information pre-exist, but self does not</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-814a-e61176b87c3f" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-940b-db39527fd8ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= total energy in region</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-aa45-c154c6001283" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= correlation structure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-8d31-eb11a67422cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">= boundary defining “self”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-88ba-c8e4ae8dde86" class="">Self emerges when a subset of DOF satisfies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-bcd2-dc67b947c497" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Persistent boundary</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bf1f-ea5e278b632e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Internal predictive closure</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-949c-eb5b6dfe72c1" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Error-corrected record accumulation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-920b-c615edca6944" class="">Formally, self exists when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-abfe-c750d1d6804b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{self} =
\mathbf{1}[
\text{closure} \wedge
\text{error-correction} \wedge
\text{control stability}
]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a29e-e0c07993dbb8" class="">Before birth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-93ef-feca5375a573" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Energy exists</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-b916-fd0f9c0e5791" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Correlations exist</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8f82-d7aae37ba566" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">But boundary not yet formed</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806a-b7f7-d0b762c53579" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">No recursive self-model depth</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-a09e-f4a844716a9b" class="">After death:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809a-82dd-d323bfc3cde2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Boundary dissolves</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-9d30-d67c6969181a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Error correction stops</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-b358-efb1aea6fca6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-8264-eafbcaf40353" class="">Energy and raw information persist, but self-organization collapses.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-8f94-eb1fe7c2b19c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-aada-c1641897058b" class="">4) The deeper overlooked layer: identity is a control invariant, not a substance</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-812b-c158927e5dc9" class="">Define identity  as a maintained invariant under system dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-bab8-d706b453f85d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}(t+1) = \mathcal{I}(t)
\quad \text{if gates satisfied}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-bb87-e3ecf1209c1f" class="">When control gates fail:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-95dd-e6cce3fd870b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists t^\* : \mathcal{I}(t^\*+1) \ne \mathcal{I}(t^\*)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-81da-f2ca121a9427" class="">Identity is lost not because energy vanished,<br/>but because invariant maintenance stopped.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-a45b-f8ba52fd1e95" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8930-eb40d2061c5b" class="">Self = maintained invariants under constrained energy flow.</blockquote></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-873d-f0180e00c32c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-b942-f6a751c10e0d" class="">5) The environment never forgets — but it does dilute</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-b35f-c6b3c660b905" class="">Let total system = organism + environment.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-bafd-df36e48a9b9a" class="">Total mutual information is conserved (unitary case), but redundancy decays:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-8532-eb23726da9e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{local}(t+1) = R_{local}(t) - \kappa \Xi R_{local}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-8ff4-c003177a6ab0" class="">Unless continually refreshed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-a998-e403cf7428af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta G_t &gt; \kappa \Xi_t R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a55b-c21862c48d88" class="">After death,  (no active maintenance),<br/>so redundancy decays exponentially.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-a26a-dba272ac499d" class="">Information exists — but no longer functionally organized.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-abe6-f9580e2828a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-95c5-c73c65e0e585" class="">6) Cross-scale: cosmic persistence vs local extinction</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-bd82-f38e9f2bbd3a" class="">Energy/information exist:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b2-953b-c56da2f957d1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">before birth (in cosmic field configuration)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-bba2-eceaf2adf9fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">during life (highly structured loop)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-9c02-e4c5ad87607a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">after death (distributed correlations)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-bfb8-d4bc272c1d69" class="">But recursion depth  only exists when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-b6ad-c3f29db2154f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P \ge kT\ln2 \cdot \dot B(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-99db-fd1cba142bc6" class="">\text{ControlGate}=1<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-b791-e97491ffe435" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{BoundaryGate}=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-a257-cff892b4606d" class="">After death:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-8d4e-f59353f70749" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P \to 0 \quad \Rightarrow \quad D \to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-a30c-f5e73feaf4ca" class="">Energy persists.<br/>Recursion does not.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9abd-c750f35d5349"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-a781-c31db854d686" class="">7) The real missing piece: temporal asymmetry of maintenance</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-846d-edd6154f3c3b" class="">Maintenance requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-a294-f0c4f3a26158" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{\Sigma} &gt; 0 \quad \text{(entropy export)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-a8d5-fefdd5a1c6d0" class="">After death:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b8b1-daabdf65a238" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{\Sigma}_{export} \to 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-bb62-c4491d28418d" class="">System equilibrates.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-9dc7-f2507e0aac38" class="">This is why the arrow matters.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-9b02-cefe94096225"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-91f1-c0978fc9ee5e" class="">8) The deepest overlooked constraint: no loop without maintenance</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-9634-e5ed832095d7" class="">Loop equation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-b253-e26d4aefcb15" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=F(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-afce-dece21a83033" class="">But stable loop requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-bf09-f3f7899be17c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F(x^\*) = x^\*
\quad \text{and} \quad
\text{perturbations damped}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-b306-cd702b3178f1" class="">Damping requires active control.<br/>Active control requires energy gradients.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-9172-ce95b46fe1b6" class="">No gradient → no loop → no self.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-9e23-d4e45d93c622" class="">Energy alone is insufficient.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-b7a4-d593abc11701"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-a1da-dff3f97a4495" class="">9) So what truly exists “before and after”?</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><table id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-a212-f2343224e624" class="simple-table"><thead class="simple-table-header"><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-9234-c6dcc63e6675"><th id="jTbc" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Layer</th><th id="elOh" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">Before birth</th><th id="rmZ&lt;" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">During life</th><th id="Qho^" class="simple-table-header-color simple-table-header">After death</th></tr></div></thead><tbody><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-ae40-ffd07ebfdd72"><td id="jTbc" class="">Energy</td><td id="elOh" class="">yes</td><td id="rmZ&lt;" class="">yes</td><td id="Qho^" class="">yes</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-9f4e-c4ac92654a33"><td id="jTbc" class="">Quantum information</td><td id="elOh" class="">yes</td><td id="rmZ&lt;" class="">yes</td><td id="Qho^" class="">yes</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-82ab-d552bbf53f29"><td id="jTbc" class="">Local organized correlations</td><td id="elOh" class="">no</td><td id="rmZ&lt;" class="">yes</td><td id="Qho^" class="">decays</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9b9b-d5fea77168de"><td id="jTbc" class="">Boundary-controlled self</td><td id="elOh" class="">no</td><td id="rmZ&lt;" class="">yes</td><td id="Qho^" class="">no</td></tr></div><div style="display:contents" dir="ltr"><tr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-9e0b-d0a956a9d293"><td id="jTbc" class="">Recursive modeling depth</td><td id="elOh" class="">no</td><td id="rmZ&lt;" class="">yes</td><td id="Qho^" class="">no</td></tr></div></tbody></table></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-b5d7-edae129a2374" class="">This is the full separation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-9d42-d768bac2f06b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9030-f00ab9d64419" class="">10) The only deeper layer left</h1></div><div <strong>loops cannot coordinate faster than causal propagation</strong>. This sets hard ceilings on:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-a28a-c06ae9ddcc15" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">governance</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-866d-c246cd6d3ec9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">markets</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-869b-e88f07371b4e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">immune response</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-ba93-dee14b96df50" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">organism coordination</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-87d7-f6f050418770" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">civilization-scale control</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-8e90-c2a13522f9ee" class="">54.1 Causal cone gate (finite speed)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-91c2-ddc88dd3c845" class="">For any influence from event  to event :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-81d6-c8644efa0624" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

future light cone, no causal update is possible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-81e2-da4a4191221b" class=""><strong>Loop implication:</strong> global dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-bcaf-e8a54ba0ada8" class="">Even if energy, memory, and locality allow a loop, it may be <strong>not compressible</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents"
