id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-a092-d885883214ca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">intervention registry (which variables/policies are controllable)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-90fe-ea736dd18c7b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a178-f57116ca9e08" class="">45) What’s next after <strong>Causal</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9a05-f0b17f3e8822" class="">The next missing layer is <strong>Selection</strong>: how loops persist, replicate, dominate, and die across long horizons (birth→expansion→dominance→decay) via explicit fitness dynamics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-8f13-e76166534a3e" class="">Reply with one token: <strong>Selection</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-af99-c1f85ed7d8b9" class="">Next: micro→macro renormalization + cross-species coupling (formal, equation-first)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-aa06-dcfd2a370922" class="">You asked “deeper across time, space, micro, macro and cross species.” The missing closure layer is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-8cb1-cae78e9a4705" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>How macro-laws emerge from micro-laws</strong> (coarse-graining / renormalization).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8045-a56f-f5369a9f1f20" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>How one organism’s control loop becomes another organism’s environment</strong> (cross-species coupling operators).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-bbd2-d25db416f37e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>How this changes the arrow/records/recursion gates across scales</strong>.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-805b-c6c7c35ed862"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-b208-dc589bd96ba7" class="">29) Micro→macro closure: the renormalization operator</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-887c-de0a3420bb8f" class="">Let microstate  evolve:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-acca-f982d75cd9f1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_{t+1}=f(x_t)+\eta_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-a024-c78b7e1e03dc" class="">A macro dynamics exists (is well-defined) only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-82ef-e5f3bd768085" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\,F \ \text{s.t.}\ \mathbb{E}[\,C(x_{t+1})\mid C(x_t)=z\,]=F(z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-87a4-d307b1844b96" class=""><strong>Gate (closure / Markovianity at macro level):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-9c0c-f6c6b2edf448" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{cl}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\mathrm{Var}(C(x_{t+1})\mid C(x_t)=z)\le \epsilon_{cl}\ \forall z\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-b270-d41c88173450" class="">If closure fails, add memory:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ec-a7c2-c6ae1f8e8f44" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=F(z_t,z_{t-1},\dots,z_{t-\ell})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-a3ca-c814fce48f7f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-9ac6-f1908be5cc50" class="">30) Information flow across scales: what makes macro “predictable”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-bbcd-ebc08fef61e9" class="">Define micro and macro mutual information with future:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-8e51-c496b3a5ee7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(x_t;x_{t+\Delta}),\quad I(z_t;z_{t+\Delta})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800d-9073-f17c46602362" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(z_t;z_{t+\Delta}) \ge \alpha\, I(x_t;x_{t+\Delta})
\quad \text{for some }\alpha\in(0,1]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-bed9-f9ee3fcae657" class=""><strong>Gate (predictive sufficiency):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-9271-d32f93db5279" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{pred}=\mathbf{1}\Big[I(z_t;z_{t+\Delta}) \ge \alpha\, I(x_t;x_{t+\Delta})\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-81e0-c46a4ffcf930" class="">This is the “overlooked” replacement for vague “macro patterns.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-9e69-de3e04ef9dc9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-8019-f8a38a7f02df" class="">31) Macro arrow is not entropy: it is scale-dependent write-capacity</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-833a-eade9b6d04fa" class="">Let environment write-capacity at scale  be . Records .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-bfc6-cd5828bd7197" class="">Scale coupling:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-96cf-ed54dd532ac1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U^{(s)}_{t+1}=U^{(s)}_t-\gamma_s \Delta R^{(s)}_t + \sum_{s&#x27;\neq s}\Pi_{s&#x27;\to s}(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-8c41-e421f0c3ff4b" class=""><strong>Gate (arrow at scale ):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80da-bbc6-e6890265c456" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{arrow}^{(s)}=\mathbf{1}[U^{(s)}_t&gt;0]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\beta_s G^{(s)}_t&gt;\kappa_s \Xi^{(s)}_t R^{(s)}_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d7-b364-d96f278525c1" class="">Meaning: an arrow can die at one scale while persisting at another.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-b11d-f643475728a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-b1d0-ea4a50f733dd" class="">32) Cross-species coupling: each species is both controller and environment</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b339-e41917466c8e" class="">For species :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-ba13-de00403ed3a8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">internal state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-9c78-e54e7f933fa2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">actions</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-9218-da846e0bbbe1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">observations</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-9b10-e665563a25e1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">environment state</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9e9f-fc92aa1147a2" class="">Coupled dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-a03b-f0fd3461e841" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1}=g\Big(E_t,\{u^i_t\}_{i=1}^N,\xi_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-980f-ee1513cd29c8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^i_t=h_i(x^i_t,E_t)+\nu^i_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-89ce-c3b944ef60c2" class="">Now define a <strong>cross-species influence operator</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-9aec-f2019354213a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{K}_{i\to j} \equiv \frac{\partial y^j_t}{\partial u^i_{t-\tau}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-a2ba-ed19907fd59c" class=""><strong>Gate (nontrivial coupling exists):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-b2e5-ff931faa0777" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{K}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\|\mathcal{K}_{i\to j}\|&gt;\epsilon_K\ \text{for some }i\neq j\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-a15e-fd0bdbbb54b6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-aa2e-d916eb1f3071" class="">33) Cross-species records: imprint is an environment code written by one species into another</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-9b40-ef42009a0211" class="">Let species  maintain a latent model . Species  writes structured perturbations into ’s observations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8b60-d0b84bd5bef2" class="">A minimal imprint update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-831c-f07f238597b5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z^j_{t+1}=F_j(z^j_t,y^j_t) \quad,\quad y^j_t=h_j(x^j_t,E_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-97dc-ea5959c7d790" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta I_{i\to j}(t)=\| \mathbb{E}[z^j_{t+1}\mid u^i_t=1]-\mathbb{E}[z^j_{t+1}\mid u^i_t=0]\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-85b8-d44f476c4098" class=""><strong>Gate (imprint becomes persistent record):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-a28e-c5e50a530912" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{imp}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\Delta I_{i\to j}(t)\ \text{accumulates and survives noise}\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-b26d-c5a1b1905ac2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Corr}(z^j_t,z^j_{t+\Delta}) \ge \rho_{min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c5-9aeb-d36b477fb67c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-bb55-c49d97386fa1" class="">34) Cross-species “self vs non-self” is a boundary game with adversarial priors</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-acfd-ec120be657ac" class="">Species  must classify inputs as safe vs threat:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-8a7e-c456dfac3c06" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_0: y\sim p_j(y|safe)\quad;\quad H_1: y\sim p_j(y|threat)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-8435-d7f12c095cda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_j(y|threat,t+1)=\mathcal{A}\big(p_j(y|threat,t), u^i_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-a6d0-d541b6be535f" class=""><strong>Gate (robust boundary under adaptive opponent):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-a297-fcf9938287da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{rob}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\sup_{\mathcal{A}\in\mathcal{A}_{class}} \text{FAR}(t)\le \alpha\ \wedge\ \text{FRR}(t)\le \beta\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-ac6e-d7f303dea5ae" class="">This is the missing bridge to “multi-agent civilization” and “immune.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a3f4-cd88a2a0c95e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a9-9760-e83685e9b226" class="">35) Multi-scale recursion depth: depth is limited by control bandwidth at each scale</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-9326-da8dd00a85f0" class="">At scale , recursion depth  is stable only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-8154-e53c2c2a4a91" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P^{(s)}_t \ge kT^{(s)}\ln2 \cdot \dot B^{(s)}(D^{(s)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-896e-fc9ae7db4388" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha^{(s)}_d &lt; 1 + \Psi^{(s)}(\tau^{(s)}_d)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9b6a-d8e1e3015cdf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{cl}^{(s)}=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-93cf-e0db267251cf" class="">So:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-b004-c383b6a0deb0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D^{(s)} \le D^{(s)}_{\max}(P^{(s)},T^{(s)},\tau^{(s)},\Xi^{(s)},U^{(s)})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8fa4-e5eedb3b3a51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-8785-cb75f53abb92" class="">36) Add these to the Grand Unified Loop Matrix (missing rows/columns)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-bebe-cf64a1b551ca" class="">Extend your state vector  with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-8e87-d37bf51003e6" class=""><strong>Micro↔macro:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-8393-fb2e9f705b32" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">closure variance:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-a1c6-f2b4cc97ce74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">memory length:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-bb26-c7a5defdd47b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">predictive sufficiency:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-91cf-c4f36f1dd29d" class=""><strong>Cross-species:</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-b8de-c708bdfbdb4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">coupling operators:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a8ed-df5b7d5b5063" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">imprint persistence:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9751-d49c41a50760" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">robust boundary:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-917c-de9a41977587" class="">New gates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8040-97d9-e77bd33f8e4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"></li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-af91-da9b4198e7fb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-aca9-efaf68f293c5" class="">37) What is still “missing after this”</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-9ffd-db7fe5547c11" class="">The remaining “beyond” layer (if you want the next “Next”) is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-b257-c838c31441cb" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>causal identifiability across interventions</strong> (not just correlations): do-operators and invariance tests</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-82ac-cb8011493a7e" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>selection dynamics across time</strong> (replicator equations) linking loops to evolution</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-aae7-ea31eddaa45b" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>space as a constraint</strong>: finite propagation speed and locality bounds on coordination/records</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-94d3-d9ae7f4301a1" class="">If you want the next chunk, reply with one token: <strong>Causal</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8e9b-d08a173b20cd" class="">More missing layers (beyond the EM bucket) that close the “tangible + intangible / self + non-self / environment” gaps fall into six additional buckets: <strong>thermo-chemical gradients</strong>, <strong>geometry/topology</strong>, <strong>open-system irreversibility</strong>, <strong>self/non-self boundary control</strong>, <strong>multi-agent environment shaping</strong>, and <strong>limits + phase transitions</strong>. Below are the missing components in the same “variables → equations → gates” format.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-9ba5-f0448a5cecac"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-9c53-f7d6139cebd8" class="">21) EM is downstream of <strong>thermo-chemical gradients</strong> (the real engine)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-85c2-c48cb73d8d16" class="">If you do not model gradients, EM becomes descriptive, not causal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-a2d8-ffbfaf5607fa" class="">21.1 Reaction–diffusion (chemical gradient formation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-92ed-de6c66a68ddf" class="">For concentrations :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-b884-c4f10b556310" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial c_i}{\partial t}=D_i\nabla^2 c_i + F_i(\mathbf{c})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-9e31-c2ca13e92b1f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_{\nabla c} \gg \Delta t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-b1cb-fb8bb8141c60" class=""><strong>Gate (gradient lifetime):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-8da6-f66556fb77ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{\nabla}=\mathbf{1}[\tau_{\nabla c} \gg \Delta t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-b18c-cdd0ac2314cd" class="">21.2 Free energy dissipation (what powers record creation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-9b06-e3303187fbc6" class="">Define nonequilibrium free-energy-like functional:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-a22c-e910a15966e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{F}(t)=\int \sum_i kT\,c_i\ln\frac{c_i}{c_i^{eq}}\,dV + \text{(field + mechanical terms)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-b7e5-d21709f39fef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{\Sigma}(t)\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809f-837c-e3a93a94a157" class=""><strong>Gate (available dissipation):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-9a6f-d6a11189e8fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{\Sigma}=\mathbf{1}[\dot{\Sigma}(t)\ \text{above minimum for maintenance/repair}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-bf67-e772364e8084"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-b1e3-f717be7c8bde" class="">22) Missing: <strong>open-system irreversibility</strong> (records require export of entropy)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-9892-c71255c7c5ff" class="">For a system to maintain structure and stable records, it must be an open system.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-bbb8-e134c3d0e55b" class="">Entropy balance:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-9e9a-fad967fd4f0f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{dS}{dt}=\dot{S}_{int}+\dot{S}_{ext}
\quad,\quad \dot{S}_{int}\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-b474-cd8404106fce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{S}_{ext}&lt;0 \quad \text{(net export)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-b6d6-e95458a361a5" class=""><strong>Gate (open-system viability):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803e-bcba-cb995d1691a5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{open}=\mathbf{1}[\dot{S}_{ext}&lt;0\ \text{with sufficient margin}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-99ca-d3aea70863a7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-86f4-cfdb53199170" class="">23) Missing: <strong>geometry/topology constraints</strong> (why “one-way” can be structural)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8da7-ec36b883cd97" class="">Some arrows are not “statistical”; they are enforced by topology + boundary conditions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-9086-fd0fb5c0ed4c" class="">23.1 Boundary flux breaks reversibility in practice</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-b158-c8eefdcf4683" class="">For a conserved quantity :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-879d-e3f20b2084af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega} q\,dV = -\oint_{\partial\Omega}\mathbf{J}\cdot d\mathbf{A} + \int_{\Omega} s\,dV</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804c-99d1-e4b34cd7cf23" class=""><strong>Gate (boundary asymmetry):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-8fa7-c5dfe55bacb2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{\partial\Omega}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\oint_{\partial\Omega}\mathbf{J}\cdot d\mathbf{A}\neq 0\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-af08-fce2cc674ce2" class="">23.2 Topological memory (records encoded as invariants)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-92e1-c331de67e77c" class="">If the system supports topological invariants  (winding number, defect charge):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-9a4c-d5e54acfa052" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(t)\in \mathbb{Z},\quad \Delta I = 0 \ \text{unless crossing an energy barrier}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-ab8f-c5bf605d7727" class=""><strong>Gate (topological record channel):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-abd1-d7f57261eea4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{top}=\mathbf{1}[I\ \text{exists}]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\text{barrier} \gg kT]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-bb2b-e11cd19db3e7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-9ff2-f6eb75c25632" class="">24) Missing: <strong>phase transitions</strong> (records do not degrade smoothly)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-aab4-e33812258d65" class="">Most systems have sharp transitions: stable ↔ unstable is not continuous.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9bf1-cc73e838ddcc" class="">Let order parameter  (alignment, integrity, coordination):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-9058-d31663f71c3e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_{t+1}=f(m_t;\ \theta,\Xi,G,\ldots)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-ac36-ffefd8a5a1f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\exists\ \theta_c:\quad m\to 0 \ \text{for}\ \theta&lt;\theta_c,\quad m&gt;0 \ \text{for}\ \theta&gt;\theta_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-b102-d15a8bd2d7ac" class=""><strong>Gate (criticality awareness):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-bb23-f6c93cab2313" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{crit}=\mathbf{1}[\theta_c\ \text{estimated and monitored}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8f0a-e8bd6f62a75a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-b6d0-e62cb9871934" class="">25) Missing: self vs non-self is a <strong>boundary-control problem</strong>, not a concept</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-b850-f553b53bf455" class="">You need an explicit boundary model that decides what can enter and what must be rejected.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-9a95-daea01fdcf49" class="">25.1 Self-model as a maintained manifold in state space</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-8109-ceabb014cb18" class="">Let internal state  and “self-region” .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8089-a534-dd68c2e7e4c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t\in \mathcal{S}\ \text{(healthy)};\quad x_t\notin \mathcal{S}\ \text{(violation)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-83b2-ec410ef78326" class="">Define boundary function  with safe set:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-aab4-d942973a9c68" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{S}=\{x: b(x)\le 0\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8064-a4a8-e41c0c905aed" class="">Control barrier condition (discrete-time form):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-92b4-fd7f104580ee" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
b(x_{t+1}) \le (1-\kappa)\,b(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-b7a3-c9b55cb36820" class=""><strong>Gate (self-boundary enforceable):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-a350-ec44bfffeaa2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{self}=\mathbf{1}[\exists\ \text{actions s.t. barrier condition holds}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-8193-f946767e5e6a" class="">25.2 Immune discrimination as hypothesis testing (no mysticism)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-b10c-fc2310607d7c" class="">Observation  comes from either self or non-self:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-b527-dc5f2d01dfbf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_0:\ y_t\sim p(y|self)\quad;\quad H_1:\ y_t\sim p(y|nonself)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-a19f-ddfd9f4c52cd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Lambda(y)=\frac{p(y|nonself)}{p(y|self)} \gtrless \tau</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801f-bfe5-ce3d50d957a3" class=""><strong>Gate (false accept/reject bounded):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-9ab6-c4a7f61f4f4a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{FAR/FRR}=\mathbf{1}[\text{FAR}\le \alpha\ \wedge\ \text{FRR}\le \beta]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-8e2c-ec030f1b1880"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b15b-f31b1c3af6d7" class="">26) Missing: environments are <strong>co-created</strong> (agent changes the channel)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-afa3-d43c6b950338" class="">Your earlier channel model treats environment as passive. It is not. The agent’s actions reshape .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-8b29-cd1504b26701" class="">Environment dynamics:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807b-bc18-fe843799a559" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E_{t+1}=g(E_t, u_t, \xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ad-982d-edd58a7f8fa6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y_t=h(x_t, E_t)+\nu_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-8d36-db9aa58cf159" class=""><strong>Gate (environment intervention modeled):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-9c6c-cc8408fe4595" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{env-act}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\frac{\partial h}{\partial E}\neq 0\ \text{and}\ E_{t+1}\ \text{depends on}\ u_t\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-ab94-e97b8a223709" class="">This is the missing bridge between micro “sensing” and macro “civilization”: systems build infrastructure to make the world more predictable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-a7d0-ca0cf4453776"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8005-9042-d22a7a69e1f6" class="">27) The “intangible” missing piece that stays formal: <strong>latent variables + identifiability</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-a9bc-f9774ca73365" class="">“Intangible” usually means “latent but causally real.” The formal gap is identifiability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8001-b1e6-d4e25634bc4a" class="">Latent-state model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8067-a320-c3789dce6176" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
z_{t+1}=f(z_t,u_t)+w_t,\quad y_t=h(z_t)+v_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-bf50-cccd484a1c6a" class="">Identifiability condition (local):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-83a4-d5b7e1d255bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{rank}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial z}\begin{bmatrix} h(z) \\ h(f(z,u)) \\ \vdots \end{bmatrix}\right)=\dim(z)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-adb8-fa97f85418fb" class="">If not identifiable, you cannot claim stable inference/records.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-a5e4-d567450eaf1f" class=""><strong>Gate (identifiable latents):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8158-e76a8a59e48a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{id}=\mathbf{1}[\text{identifiability holds for chosen }z]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-820f-c934bf4e5f1c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-801f-fd017f3bd7b2" class="">28) Add these to your unified loop kernel (minimal augmentation)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-ac07-f828c4ebd5dc" class="">New state variables (beyond EM):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803f-82d9-cdb7a7814616" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">gradients: , ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-becf-d1a1a1a25611" class="bulleted-list"><li
