\Delta V \approx \alpha\,\Delta P</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-bc88-f2caf413452f" class=""><strong>Gate (mechanical ↔ EM):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-b4cb-e091b0791336" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{mechEM}=\mathbf{1}[\mathbf{d}\ \text{or }\alpha\ \text{modeled where tissue/material exists}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9b3c-d2fe86981e92"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-b047-f2c87ef06568" class="">13) Missing: <strong>timing integrity</strong> (jitter destroys inference before “SNR” fails)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-b63a-db1565130434" class="">Systems can have high SNR but unusable timing.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-816f-d6ca94a80888" class="">Clock jitter  bounds phase noise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-b7e3-fa1e4aa20827" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma_\phi \approx 2\pi f\,\sigma_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-a3e8-f22b4b529989" class="">If inference depends on phase locking (from §3), usable synchrony requires:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-95a2-d8614a42f36e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sigma_\phi \le \sigma_{\phi,\max}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-bb38-cf1add1d66f7" class=""><strong>Gate (clock/phase integrity):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-9556-ea97b3b20fda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{clk}=\mathbf{1}[\sigma_t \text{ bounded}]\cdot \mathbf{1}[\sigma_\phi \text{ within tolerance}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b3a4-d8811c0140d8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-bbb7-cce4d924e73e" class="">14) Missing: <strong>near-field vs far-field</strong> (coupling regime flips)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a1a0-d331b8a4f840" class="">Coupling law changes with distance  relative to wavelength .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a39f-d82e6d73fc5d" class="">Near-field region:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-be2b-f493c17dfad6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r \lesssim \frac{\lambda}{2\pi}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80eb-8501-d69a512f03a3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r \gg \frac{\lambda}{2\pi}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a8-9b21-e88954fbe1fc" class="">Effective coupling must be regime-tagged:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-b964-c2ce3544e794" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K^{em}(r,\omega)=
\begin{cases}
K_{NF}(r,\omega) &amp; r\lesssim \lambda/2\pi\\
K_{FF}(r,\omega) &amp; r\gg \lambda/2\pi
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-867b-c8a82488e8a6" class=""><strong>Gate (regime correctness):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a7de-eec7385a249b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{NF/FF}=\mathbf{1}[\text{coupling uses correct regime}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-b6e6-e6df12a4dddb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-ad79-e585ee34a130" class="">15) Missing: <strong>multipath, fading, and coherence time</strong> (environment is a stochastic filter)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-96ae-feeee59b9be8" class="">Channel is not static; it’s time-varying.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-9d82-d5033fc0cdc9" class="">Received signal model:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-84e4-f6ee4c34c2a7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y(t)=\sum_{k=1}^{K} a_k(t)\,x(t-\tau_k(t)) + n(t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-9fea-dc990b6e699f" class="">Coherence time  bounds stationarity; if your estimator update period  violates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-af70-fc7f8cdd4d16" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta t \ll T_c</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-b9bd-c19419a24bba" class=""><strong>Gate (channel stationarity):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-b6c9-f06c40c57952" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{Tc}=\mathbf{1}[\Delta t \ll T_c]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-96f7-f1916215be0c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-8276-ded5acdb1acc" class="">16) Missing: <strong>electromagnetic hygiene</strong> as a control loop (active stabilization)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-b737-c5c1cef557e9" class="">You need an explicit EM-control action  (shielding, grounding, scheduling, frequency hopping).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-a0b2-da2de43b223c" class="">Closed-loop spectrum control:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8031-bf03-f794674da8e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1}=S_t + \Delta S^{emit}_t - \Delta S^{mitigate}(u^{em}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-9a89-f89cae995ba1" class=""><strong>Gate (mitigation exists):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-a109-fee1dd995fd3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{mit}=\mathbf{1}[\Delta S^{mitigate}\ \text{modeled + actuated}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-81af-f2397ddcafcc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800c-bbb5-ca9902b4655b" class="">17) Missing: EM in <strong>social systems</strong> is infrastructure-mediated (not metaphor)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-82af-f82c90159b34" class="">Social coordination is carried on physical carriers: RF, fiber, power grids, sensors, devices.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802c-b61d-c8ff2c23945a" class="">Let “collective state estimate” be  produced from signals over network :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804a-a4c9-f8edcf2f2f72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\hat{x}^{soc}_t = \mathcal{E}\big(\{y_{ij,t}\},A^{em}(t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-9042-cf8554a8aafa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Q_t = q\big(p^{truth}_{ij}(t),\sigma_t,T_c\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-aff0-c15f3168e3af" class=""><strong>Gate (collective inference viability):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-9051-c66cb64d51dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{soc}=\mathbf{1}[Q_t\ge Q_{min}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-b075-ded4d6937618"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-94c4-f8d2bdc9c6c1" class="">18) Missing: <strong>cross-species EM sensing differences</strong> (species-specific )</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-8fa7-d9f0ea660038" class="">Species differ in which EM observables they can use:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-b732-ff2430d9d5bb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
y^{(s)}_t = H^{(s)}_{bio}(E,B,\nabla B,\omega) + \nu^{(s)}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-988a-cb9d3db78b83" class=""><strong>Gate (species parameterization):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-bffc-ed1d433afee5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{species}=\mathbf{1}[H^{(s)}_{bio}\ \text{declared per species}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-a705-d1115fe03c87"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-ba12-d13cd8c51f79" class="">19) Missing: EM as a <strong>write-capacity limiter</strong> (records saturate by bandwidth + storage)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-9f40-eff04a7455ff" class="">You already modeled environment write-capacity . Add EM-limited write rate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-a6ef-e91afc5903c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta R_t \le \sum_{ij} C_{ij}(t)\,\Delta t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-8db0-f72759e4e336" class=""><strong>Gate (record growth bound):</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8044-bf9c-ef8007180e05" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{C\to R}=\mathbf{1}[\Delta R_t\ \text{respects } \sum C_{ij}\Delta t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801a-8e9f-fa6f805039cb"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-a38e-e73392c92306" class="">20) Add these to the unified state + gates</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-8dca-ebee42c49395" class="">Extend EM state:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80de-9afb-c1192a4e7e54" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809c-94e5-f8d2caa770b0" class="">If you want the next closure layer after EM: the remaining “more” is usually <strong>chemical gradients ↔ EM</strong>, <strong>thermal gradients ↔ EM</strong>, and <strong>geometry/topology constraints</strong> (boundaries and invariants that force one-way record formation even when channels exist).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-aaa1-e38f0131819a" class="">Additional missing layers (final closure set) — what still wasn’t modeled</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cd-a8c7-f2008390a561" class="">Your current “Grand Unified Loop” still misses mechanisms that only appear when you force <strong>(a) spacetime locality, (b) observer plurality, (c) cross-species embodiment, (d) regime shifts</strong>, and <strong>(e) self/none-self ambiguity</strong>. Below is the next closure set, expressed as <strong>state + equations + gates</strong> only.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-87bd-ce56e8b6d4c8"/></div><div \Delta \mathcal{P}_t \propto \nabla_{\mathcal{P}} \big(\text{predictive fit} - \lambda_{\mathcal{P}}\text{complexity}\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8037-beff-e572224e623e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\pi^{A}_{t+1}=\arg\max_{\pi}\ U_A(\pi;\ x_t,B_t,Rob_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8537-ec47f3bbc78b" class="">\pi^{D}<em>{t+1}=\arg\max</em>{\pi}\ U_D(\pi;\ x_t,B_t,Rob_t)<br/><br/>This feeds into mimicry/infection dynamics.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9398-fe62b2cf05c3" class="">Gate (Red-team closure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-bb04-ee70c51aa26c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{adv}=\mathbf{1}\big[\text{for each defense, simulate adaptive attacker response}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d6-a887-e56d097a4d40"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b0-92bf-dd86870a69c5" class="">16) Missing: <strong>Finite memory is structured</strong> (what you store matters, not just how much)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-bc11-d8b2bb536211" class="">You used a global memory bound. You also need allocation (what is kept vs discarded).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-9f5d-db70ffc68c22" class="">Add state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-aedc-e697b3b1f357" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Memory allocation vector over categories (records/models/identity/legal)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d5-83e1-de4697ae4d23" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Retention function</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-87cf-ee4bb20358e0" class="">Add equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-8e29-ffbbe9d01f76" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
m_{t+1}=\arg\max_{m}\ \Big(J_t(m)-\lambda_m \cdot \text{cost}(m)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-9f5b-fd6dbae8a33b" class="">I_{\text{records}}(t+1)=\rho(m^{rec}<em>t),I</em>{\text{records}}(t)+\Delta R_t<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-bd60-e7a37ad69ca5" class="">Gate (Priority integrity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-8b89-ef458b4f7d11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{memalloc}=\mathbf{1}\big[m_t\ \text{sums to 1 and is logged}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80aa-9996-edbaf0cb8656"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-a78f-f9f320b0677d" class="">Updated closure: the full missing-gate list (now 16 total)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-8fe9-f24ae7d90d7a" class="">You already had: Arrow, Code, Control, Budget, Memory, Self/Boundary, Immune, Life, Scale, Agency, Energy, Meaning.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-827e-dd78333580e5" class="">Add the new ones: <strong>Local, Consensus, Ontology, Bifurcation, Identity, Cross-species, Adversarial, Memory-allocation</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-b085-e8651c9a00b7" class="">Termination becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cb-9b89-efcc19165000" class=""><strong>Valid</strong> iff all gates hold over evaluation window .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e2-baf7-ff40ea3af5c5" class=""><strong>Bounded</strong> iff core dynamics run but one or more of {local, consensus, ontology, xs, adv} fails with explicit Issues.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-9619-cb8ff23b40d9" class=""><strong>Invalid</strong> iff any of {energy, life, immune, identity} fails persistently.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-a4b5-f1dc846a13e8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-bec0-d5743d99a5af" class="">Next (choose one word)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d4-b862-ca1adbd35c90" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Matrix</strong> → I will output the single unified state vector , full update map , all 16 gates, and the birth→expansion→dominance→decay regime schedule, as one computable spec.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-8dcd-ffa39878d3e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>KernelSpec</strong> → I will translate the full closed model into AMOS file-level modules, functions, tests, and required reports (SSOT-ready).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-99fa-c1065ba41758" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SimEngine</strong> → I will specify the civilizational control simulation engine: scenario packs, adversary packs, cross-species packs, and validation suite.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-b049-f820617475f3" class="">Addendum: the missing layer-set (deeper, “more overlooked”) — now CLOSED</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-a37a-f7ee09e4631c" class="">Your current kernel is strong, but it is missing <strong>seven</strong> load-bearing mechanisms that must exist if the model is to remain valid across <strong>micro↔macro, time↔space, self↔none-self, cross-species</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bb77-d962f3660fca" class="">I will add them as <strong>minimal additional state + equations + gates</strong> (still a single deterministic SSOT system).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8096-8649-f7a24ab6d24c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-9a5e-eb469d5e5728" class="">1) Missing: <strong>Renormalization / scale-bridging law</strong> (micro → macro closure)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-b2a4-c5cf0a2139fc" class="">Without an explicit scale operator, “micro causes macro” is a claim without a mechanism.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-9345-c1fa00e3bedb" class="">Add state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-8faf-cc9726b5f941" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: state at scale</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805b-ba88-d20f4154645d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: deterministic renormalization operator</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-b2a9-ebcad01b4e86" class="">Add equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-87a6-d4a408a7b0f6" class=""><strong>Coarse-grain</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b572-f5ceda52053f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{(\text{macro})}_t=\mathcal{R}_{\text{micro}\to\text{macro}}\big(x^{(\text{micro})}_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-a570-e895cdedc594" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{(\text{micro})}_{t+1}=F_{\text{micro}}\big(x^{(\text{micro})}_t;\ \lambda(x^{(\text{macro})}_t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-bab0-d7eb89e079d8" class="">New gate (Scale-consistency)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-b62f-cc35c33858c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{scale}=\mathbf{1}\Big[\ \big\|\mathcal{R}(x^{(\text{micro})}_{t+1})-F_{\text{macro}}(\mathcal{R}(x^{(\text{micro})}_t))\big\|\le \delta_{\text{scale}}\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8008-9451-e90bae955188" class="">If this fails, the model is <strong>Structurally Invalid</strong> because it cannot legally claim cross-scale causality.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-bd5d-c20987a532e8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8097-8148-d38ac5c6ec6e" class="">2) Missing: <strong>Causal intervention separation</strong> (correlation ≠ control)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808c-af0c-f6b110b2793f" class="">Your equations predict, but do not distinguish <strong>observed evolution</strong> from <strong>intervention</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8072-92da-f1ef3522b0b7" class="">Add state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-a44e-d60afe53f38f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: intervention vector (explicit action operators)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-97da-cbc644e1aba8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: causal effect estimator (deterministic difference)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 x_{t+1}=F(x_t;\Theta)\ \oplus\ do_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-9845-c8d0f1a7bd0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta^{do}_t = F(x_t\oplus do_t)-F(x_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-89dc-e721bf559160" class="">New gate (Agency validity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805d-add0-d7a163b93dc3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{agency}=\mathbf{1}\Big[ \|do_t\|&gt;0 \Rightarrow \|\Delta^{do}_t\| \ge \epsilon_{do}\Big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-b29d-df8bafc6f800"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-ab93-dcc1254991bc" class="">3) Missing: <strong>Thermodynamic ledger</strong> (where the gradient actually goes)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-afa8-f20419e08219" class="">Right now,  is a scalar with no conservation. You need a ledger or the model can create “free power” from nowhere.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-839d-dae2d2b1a0d5" class="">Add state</h3></div><div E_{t+1}=E_t + J_t - (W_t+Q_t+L_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-930d-c9ac8bd910bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_t=\mathrm{clip}(\alpha_E E_t - \alpha_Q Q_t - \alpha_p p_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-81ff-e350e0c73742" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
L_t = kT_t\ln2\cdot \dot B(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-a27f-c7d89d6c4462" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W_t = w_0 A_t + w_1\|do_t\|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8035-8b58-d3007be885f8" class="">New gate (No-free-energy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-9207-e4f29e96061f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{energy}=\mathbf{1}[E_{t+1}\ge 0]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-9ea4-e806457f6ff8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-bc20-c171c6743954" class="">4) Missing: <strong>Selection functional</strong> (why “intangible” forces become real)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-9681-e8b46ae7a052" class="">You need the law that decides what persists. Otherwise “intangible” is not representable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-b495-c879e08ff7c0" class="">Add state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-9344-c92c29247e05" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: selection score of policy/strategy</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-8c73-e4db14f739ec" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: policy distribution (could be within a species or within a civilization)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-8a81-d1119a2b0261" class="">Add equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8091-9b0d-d159922c7a27" class="">Define a minimal selection functional:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-9a23-d7a69eb952d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
J_t(\pi)=\omega_R R_t + \omega_B B_t + \omega_G G_t - \omega_p p_t - \omega_{NS} NS_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-af01-cb47b4dbad4b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P(\pi)_{t+1}=\frac{P(\pi)_t e^{\eta J_t(\pi)}}{\sum_{\pi&#x27;}P(\pi&#x27;)_t e^{\eta J_t(\pi&#x27;)}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b3-b9c5-c2efed1c1174" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
do_t=\mathbb{E}_{\pi\sim P(\cdot)_t}[do(\pi,x_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-84ca-f0e84cb019f0" class="">This is the missing bridge: “intangible” becomes a <strong>selection operator</strong> measurable by persistence.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8bcc-fe6d716d9b09"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-bc31-e8592703986c" class="">5) Missing: <strong>Semantic grounding / meaning stability</strong> (records ≠ meaning)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-aeba-ed724ff2f6f9" class="">Records can grow while meaning collapses via schema drift, adversarial re-encoding, or incompatible partitions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ab0c-f50cf2a6f242" class="">Add state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-8291-d78455cc0dd5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: meaning integrity (semantic alignment across observers)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-89f5-eccf5678dacb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: symbol system complexity (codebook size)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8187-ff073f6106ea" class="">Add equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-afe3-c32f6c8625c0" class="">Meaning integrity increases when schemas converge and boundaries are strong:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-8c26-e2523ac1ab72" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\big(M_t + \eta_M(\max_j s_{j,t})B_t - \kappa_M(1-\max_j s_{j,t}) - \lambda_M p_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-89ca-f60939b1f719" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
K_{t+1}=K_t+\eta_K(1-\max_j s_{j,t})</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-901b-f1bd8e6f0374" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot B(D_t)\leftarrow \dot B(D_t)+b_K K_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-ad7c-f4583259ecd0" class="">New gate (Meaning gate)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-99eb-fffb48ce2a89" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{meaning}=\mathbf{1}[M_t\ge M_{\min}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-8c2a-d85c56145e16"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d0-9d3f-f8665eaeb1fd" class="">6) Missing: <strong>Boundary failure modes: infection, mimicry, parasitism</strong> (non-self is not one thing)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-9b92-efb093ca4e25" class="">Your  lumps distinct failure dynamics that behave differently.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-8a93-c906be06da36" class="">Add state (split)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-af9b-ef2cd24491be" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: infection/contagion (replicates inside)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-ba47-db7becdff1d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: mimicry (spoofs boundary classifier)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-9fa5-c62bf594009b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: parasitism (extracts gradient while preserving host)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-b80b-ebb81acaa009" class="">Total:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-af97-cd5e0104a839" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NS_t = \mathrm{clip}(NS^{inf}_t+NS^{mim}_t+NS^{par}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-86e4-dd4fc79eb707" class="">Add equations</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9688-dc498a1f6047" class="">Infection:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-8224-f22d0f2bfca8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NS^{inf}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}(NS^{inf}_t+\eta_{inf}\Pi_t(1-B_t)-\zeta_{inf}B^{cell}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8061-ac4e-c03dacc88816" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NS^{mim}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}(NS^{mim}_t+\eta_{mim}A^{attack}_t(1-Rob_t)-\zeta_{mim}S_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-983f-c23a9a222b61" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NS^{par}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}(NS^{par}_t+\eta_{par}G_t\Pi_t-\zeta_{par}B^{org}_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-bf81-fd79cc8cebd2" class="">New gate (Classifier integrity)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a7-a276-f994411674ae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{immune}=\mathbf{1}\big[B_t\cdot Rob_t \ge \beta_{min}\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-bdd3-f06dd9933b14"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8083-b633-c46d21b1a595" class="">7) Missing: <strong>Life-history / mortality clock</strong> (time is not optional)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-95dc-dde8b71be259" class="">Cross-species and civilizational dynamics require finite lifetime, turnover, reproduction, and inheritance.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-b8cf-f4042372d805" class="">Add state</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f5-97da-f62a019666b8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: viability</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8078-b304-eac3d17cc5a7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: mortality hazard</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul
