href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-9db5-f71aee65633c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1}= \mathrm{clip}\Big(q_t - \eta_q \mathcal{W}_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-9fbf-ed21f1313f2b" class="">2.2 Weyl activation grows as constraints unwind + structure forms</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-b10e-ec7501043b48" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{W}_{t+1}= \mathrm{clip}\Big(\mathcal{W}_t + \eta_W(1-q_t) - \zeta_W G_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-bdd0-c859f134e6ee" class="">2.3 Predictability bandwidth (sensing quality)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-80fd-e2eace3b02b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{t+1}= \mathrm{clip}\Big(S_t + \eta_S q_t - \kappa_S \mathcal{W}_t - \lambda_S T_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a3bd-feda33727e8c" class="">2.4 Boundary integrity (immune classifier with delay + attack)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a884-d146bb3be1b3" class="">Define effective delay . Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-9047-f0b2de30df6f" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1}= \mathrm{clip}\Big(\sigma(\alpha_B S_t - \beta_B \tau_t - \gamma_B p_t) \cdot Rob_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-b14b-e29c09e90777" class="">2.5 Robustness vs targeted deception</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-90ee-e9eb9daeb157" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Rob_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(Rob_t + \eta_{Rob}(1-A^{attack}_t) - \kappa_{Rob}\Pi_t(1-B_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804b-ad29-f85927fba4ec" class="">2.6 Deception pressure (endogenous + exogenous)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-93e7-ccbb04889c37" class="">Deception increases when coalitional selection rewards it (see  below) and when records are valuable:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-8230-e8f2fb981c52" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A^{attack}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(A^{attack}_t + \eta_A Z^{coalition}_t R_t - \zeta_A B_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-a7b5-c4511d5387da" class="">2.7 Permeability (healthy openness requires strong boundary)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8289-ce56ec4c499e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(\Pi_t + \eta_\Pi G_t B_t - \kappa_\Pi p_t - \lambda_\Pi(1-B_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-8b8a-e79dd2e5a984" class="">2.8 Noise rate (includes non-self overwrite + targeted attack)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-a06c-ee29ad5cc408" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(p_t + p^{env}_t + \underbrace{\Pi_t(1-B_t)\Omega_t}_{\text{non-self overwrite}} + \underbrace{A^{attack}_t(1-Rob_t)}_{\text{targeted deception}}\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e0-8439-fc3523adba17" class="">Where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8014-9b85-e2ac0c959be5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega_t = c_1(1-\Sigma_t)+c_2(1-Z^{truth}_t)+c_3 Z^{coalition}_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9c20-ca6a614ace25" class="">2.9 Gradient (now includes agency reshaping)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-a95d-f5a6d506f11e" class="">Base gradient grows from low constraint unwind + available write capacity + predictability; it is harmed by noise and temperature:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-a50d-f50edc4a9be7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(G_t + \eta_G q_t S_t U_t + \eta_{GA}A_t - \kappa_G p_t - \lambda_G T_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a2b9-ef04812626e6" class="">2.10 Agency (actions require stable self and sufficient gradient)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-a0e2-ee56d4a8b398" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
A_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(A_t + \eta_A^\star C_t G_t - \kappa_A NS_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-b6b9-e7828f5f2d53" class="">2.11 Record redundancy (code distance proxy)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8041-b6e9-f13136816ecc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(r_t + \eta_r G_t - \kappa_r p_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-aa69-e1e91911b6ec" class="">2.12 Record update (with coding threshold + reversibility)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-bd96-c74a02a36d82" class="">Let  (monotone increasing).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f6-905d-eb960a2921ad" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(R_t + \beta G_t B_t U_t - \kappa p_t R_t - \lambda \mathbf{1}[p_t\ge p_{th}(r_t)]R_t + \rho_t \Delta^-R_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-bbfd-fad8e00b7662" class="">2.13 Environment write-capacity depletion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8046-8f42-fac14bd7ce20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}= \mathrm{clip}\Big(U_t - \gamma (R_{t+1}-R_t)_+\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-ba95-e5af3e5c5fef" class="">2.14 Reversibility window (mixing kills reversibility)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-9c83-c32e89b27731" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\rho_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(\rho_t + \eta_\rho q_t - \kappa_\rho \mathcal{W}_t - \lambda_\rho R_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-a3b4-c10913c4a882" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Delta^-R_t = -\min\{R_t,\; \xi \rho_t p_t\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-85c9-f348a3766cab" class="">2.15 Recursion depth and error dynamics (control-delay bounded)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-a388-e82b891d4883" class="">Error per level:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-81c6-f65da6e20cc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\varepsilon^{(d)}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(\alpha_d \varepsilon^{(d)}_t + \eta_d p_t + \eta&#x27;_d T_t - r^{(d)}_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-9821-e5907e2809e4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{d=1}^{D_{\max}} r^{(d)}_t \le \chi G_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-8527-d79854964ad7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r^{(d)}_t = \chi G_t \cdot \frac{\omega^{d-1}}{\sum_{k=1}^{D_{\max}}\omega^{k-1}} \cdot \mathbf{1}[d\le D_t]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806b-b028-de74d6db7d28" class="">Depth update requires stability + budget + memory:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c4-9b76-eee15121f2f4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=
\begin{cases}
\min(D_t+1,D_{\max}) &amp; \text{if } \max_{d\le D_t}\varepsilon^{(d)}_t \le \epsilon \ \land\ Gate_{budget}=1\ \land\ Gate_{memory}=1\\
\max(D_t-1,0) &amp; \text{if } \max_{d\le D_t}\varepsilon^{(d)}_t &gt; \epsilon\\
D_t &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-8073-cff8b62ab4f4" class="">2.16 Self-consistency (identity stability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-bb79-feb9c9c06bb5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t = \mathrm{clip}\Big(1-\sum_{d=1}^{D_{\max}} w_d \varepsilon^{(d)}_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a2-9e40-de450ea351e8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{IdentityBreak}_t \iff C_t &lt; C_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-a80c-c1a9a3bfb9bb" class="">2.17 None-self attractor (collapse regime)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-a445-f125b2e4d66a" class="">None-self rises when boundary fails + noise dominates + recursion collapses:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8099-bd16-e2b6170107d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
NS_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(NS_t + \eta_{NS}(1-B_t)p_t + \eta&#x27;_{NS}(C_{\min}-C_t)_+ - \zeta_{NS}G_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8095-89cb-e7aac674095d" class="">2.18 Synchrony / coordination quality (macro cohesion, replaces handwavy “social glue”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-840c-e12c60667b1e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(\Sigma_t + \eta_\Sigma B^{group}_t R_t - \kappa_\Sigma p_t - \lambda_\Sigma NS_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b990-e6f1fa4fae57" class="">2.19 Multi-scale boundary nesting (consistency constraints)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-a5c5-de0a4ca56f30" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B^{cell}_{t+1}=f_{cell}(S_t,p_t,T_t),\quad
B^{org}_{t+1}=f_{org}(B^{cell}_t,C_t),\quad
B^{group}_{t+1}=f_{group}(B^{org}_t,\Sigma_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-bdde-ea05ffb44dc3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B^{cell}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\big(\sigma(a_c S_t-b_c p_t-c_c T_t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-9119-db8b6c0a6b9c" class="">B^{org}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\big(\sigma(a_o B^{cell}_t-b_o(1-C_t))\big)<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-846b-f50f8c626a77" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B^{group}_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\big(\sigma(a_g B^{org}_t-b_g p_t-c_g NS_t)\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-8725-c0227ef344d0" class="">2.20 Observer-relative coarse-graining (multiple “arrows”)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-bf79-cc538030dfab" class="">Each observer sees records through their partition . Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801d-b358-e6e863b56f10" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R^{(i)}_t = \Phi(C_i, R_t, p_t, r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-a5fb-db504811a9dd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R^{(i)}_t=\mathrm{clip}\big(\upsilon_i R_t - \chi_i p_t\big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-b1dc-f5b9dda00fe7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R^{(i)}_{t+1}-R^{(i)}_t &gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-adaf-c4d084cff717" class="">2.21 Schema ecology (competing record standards)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-b2bf-d2cff33e38bb" class="">Fitness of schema :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8029-b45c-f8bd4f801b52" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F_{j,t}= a_j G_t + b_j B_t - c_j p_t - d_j \mathrm{Incompat}(j)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801e-9ace-f75fa98a18bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
s_{j,t+1}=\frac{s_{j,t}\exp(\lambda F_{j,t})}{\sum_k s_{k,t}\exp(\lambda F_{k,t})}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a00c-c7b16e7546df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p^{env}_t \leftarrow p^{env}_t + \kappa_{schema}\Big(1-\max_j s_{j,t}\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8012-9ffb-eccc9e59db41" class="">2.22 Hysteresis (path dependence)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805c-8724-eac070354f9f" class="">Hysteresis accumulates when identity breaks or schema conflict is high:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-9f5c-e4c45998b616" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(H_t + \eta_H \mathbf{1}[\text{IdentityBreak}_t] + \eta&#x27;_H(1-\max_j s_{j,t}) - \zeta_H B_t\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-ba12-f131ae31e9fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{t+1}\leftarrow \mathrm{clip}(B_{t+1}-\kappa_H H_t),\quad
\epsilon \leftarrow \epsilon(1-\kappa&#x27;_H H_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8918-f698cd341f43" class="">2.23 Spatial transport (local-to-global closure)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-801b-a51b-d776f3d1b465" class="">All scalar fields can be node-indexed . Example for records:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b4-ad74-fe32d9caeb97" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}(v)=\mathrm{clip}\Big(R_t(v)+\beta G_t(v)B_t(v)U_t(v)-\kappa p_t(v)R_t(v)\Big)+(\mathcal{D}R_t)(v)+(\mathcal{A}R_t)(v)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-9550-fa7edf5bbb78" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t=\frac{1}{|V|}\sum_v R_t(v)\quad (\text{same for }G,U,p,B)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-bc81-da323f540a02" class="">2.24 Endogenous temperature / substrate agitation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-8b3a-d41d4ea87167" class="">Temperature rises with high activity + conflict; falls with stability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8057-a213-cd8f0e0da0b1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}\Big(T_t + \eta_T (A_t+p_t+NS_t) - \zeta_T (B_t+\Sigma_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-ad3a-c556126fa849"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8039-8eba-f235aa4592c2" class="">3) Gates (hard failure modes)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-9b95-f636b000825c" class="">Define binary gates evaluated each step.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-9676-e0437e91b027" class="">Gate 1: ArrowGate (records can grow)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-b770-d1c8b56be5b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{arrow}= \mathbf{1}\big[\beta G_t B_t U_t &gt; \kappa p_t R_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-b483-ce4d2e30fd8e" class="">Gate 2: CodeGate (below threshold)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8055-a093-e063102d6e20" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{code}= \mathbf{1}[p_t &lt; p_{th}(r_t)]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b69d-d28c60233c64" class="">Gate 3: ControlGate (delay-stable recursion)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-b055-f868935ca17d" class="">Let . Stability proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a98a-c6b321aba5d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{control}= \mathbf{1}\big[S_t &gt; s_0 + s_1 \tau_t\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-99d0-d569ad1e268c" class="">Gate 4: BudgetGate (Landauer + compute)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-a181-f142dc482fb9" class="">Define bit-erasure rate proxy . Available power proxy . Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8034-8d43-d9ebc6b3aabf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{budget}=\mathbf{1}\big[P_t \ge kT_t\ln2 \cdot \dot B(D_t)\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-9316-ec1cf8b24be3" class="">Gate 5: MemoryGate (horizon/write budget)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c7-a3fd-d2d142b416ab" class="">Let record memory  and model memory . Capacity .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-bd2a-fa65b8715d4a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{memory}=\mathbf{1}\big[I_{rec}(R_t,r_t)+I_{mod}(D_t) \le I_{\max}(U_t)\big]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-b87c-e32c2e6a3f9a" class="">Gate 6: SelfGate (identity stable)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c6-a460-f6c3fd15ade2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{self}= \mathbf{1}[C_t\ge C_{\min}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-a55f-e7d942934f6d" class="">Gate 7: BoundaryGate (self/non-self separation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-a370-d3b163af9585" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{boundary}= \mathbf{1}[B_t\ge B_{\min}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-b358-f3b563b011dd" class="">Gate 8: NoneSelfGate (avoid collapse attractor)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-b2a7-f7307dddea11" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Gate_{ns}= \mathbf{1}[NS_t&lt;NS_{\max}]</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-a8a8-f51bf9b748d1" class="">Termination classification at time :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-bc72-eb14715a6985" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Valid</strong> if all gates 1–8 hold for a sustained window .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8345-f17322fb6c8d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Bounded</strong> if ArrowGate holds but one of {Budget, Memory, Control} fails.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Expansion</strong>:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-8d7a-e5982a71c1ae" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e6-a667-f923039a8f30"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b9-8f27-c83e88203056" class="">5) Cross-species (closed mapping, same kernel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-ac8f-c54965fe61ad" class="">Each species  is the same system with parameter pack  and potentially different Coupling is via shared spatial environment , gradients , and schema ecology .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-b2f4-e95145e9078c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-ad9d-d2d61ef873d9" class="">No placeholder concepts remain; all are state variables or operators.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808f-8fab-f563d1882c4f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-8875-e1b1d2512625" class="">Next output (pick one word)</h2></div><div class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SimEngine</strong> → simulation engine design: scenarios, parameter packs, calibration, outputs, validation suite.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ed-9a76-fe559fbce9b9" class="">Add the missing layer: Self / Non-Self boundary + Tangible / Intangible as a single coupled system</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-ba62-d655e22528f8" class="">The major missing piece is an explicit <strong>boundary operator</strong> that decides what counts as “self” vs “non-self” at each scale (cell → organism → group → civilization) and how that boundary controls records, gradients, and conflict.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804e-8a1c-fdc8c77c593f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bb-8d1d-cfcef0546948" class="">1) Add two new state variables</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-b50e-cfb7d1389a4d" class="">Extend the state vector with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8022-8311-ec42c2336ea7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x_t \leftarrow
\begin{bmatrix}
\cdots\\
B_t\\
\Pi_t
\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-a22e-ca0c30139f20" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>Boundary integrity</strong> (how reliably the system separates self/non-self)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bd-9aa1-d6211414b6ef" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>Permeability</strong> (how much non-self is allowed into the self-model)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-b230-e3d446550ff0" class="">These two are independent. High permeability with high boundary integrity = healthy exchange; high permeability with low boundary integrity = contamination.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-855b-fa35d18bee7a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dc-ba8f-e485609cb225" class="">2) Define Self / Non-Self formally (no metaphors)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806e-a300-e914183cb040" class="">Let the system maintain a generative model .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802f-ac43-dd2a7b4f15e2" class="">Define instantaneous prediction error:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-b6e2-d1b0faf9bfa3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
e_t = -\log p_\theta(o_{t+1}\mid s_t,a_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e8-b26c-cf19453fe0da" class="">Partition incoming influence into two streams:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-85b5-ddb3ee608fa1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Self-consistent stream</strong> (explained by the current model)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8085-9d11-d6dcf4b8411c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Non-self stream</strong> (residual not explained)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-b197-c165c69028f6" class="">Define non-self mass:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8066-846a-f3c6116931c3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
N_t = \mathbb{E}[e_t] \quad \text{(normalized to }[0,1]\text{)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-baac-ef0cc8862633" class="">Define self mass as the complement:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-bf6e-e2779494844d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t = 1 - N_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-8907-ca38dbe0f6b6" class="">So “non-self” is not philosophical; it is <strong>model-external residual</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-a918-da08ed2dca3d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803a-a541-ffc58627bbd0" class="">3) Boundary integrity is an immune-style classifier (micro → macro)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-a015-e94f9cf02f8e" class="">Boundary integrity  is the system’s ability to correctly label residuals as:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-b3c0-cd8e11f0c2cb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">harmless novelty (learn),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-85bd-ca51874e6246" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">threat (reject),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-96c9-cf82861a3783" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">noise (ignore),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-97f2-ed1b4a892855" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">deception (counteract).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-bfff-fdcc0943e58b" class="">Model  as a function of discrimination quality  (signal detection):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-83ed-f401fe73d091" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d&#x27;_t = \frac{\mu_{\text{self}} - \mu_{\text{nonself}}}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\text{self}}^2+\sigma_{\text{nonself}}^2}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802d-9f2c-f6098b36c392" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-b286-c2d0534d955c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t = \sigma(\alpha_B d&#x27;_t - \beta_B \tau_t - \gamma_B \Xi_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8058-9d59-e205305377a2" class="">Overlooked closure: <strong>control delay</strong>  directly damages boundary integrity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807f-9c26-ff5db9c60f78"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806d-ac0d-dcc5cc583fcc" class="">4) Tangible vs intangible becomes “observable vs latent drivers” (explicit)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-baa4-e7000b575535" class="">Split drivers into:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800e-8eee-e5945bff0858" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Tangible drivers : energy, bandwidth, substrate, explicit incentives.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-8436-c6605a350e22" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Intangible drivers : latent selection pressures, memetic fitness, reputational survival, narrative reward.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-ad12-f10343f04a5e" class="">You already had a latent term . Make it a vector:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8796-ce886930b4ca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_t =
\begin{bmatrix}
Z_t^{\text{survival}}\\
Z_t^{\text{status}}\\
Z_t^{\text{coalition}}\\
Z_t^{\text{truth}}\\
Z_t^{\text{profit}}
\end{bmatrix}
\in[0,1]^5</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-a2e2-da9b30c73288" class="">These are not “values”; they are <strong>measured selection signals</strong> inferred from what persists.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ab-a824-d5bc94018277" class="">Update each component from persistence differentials:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800a-a693-dd82ea88699a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_{t+1}^{(k)} = \mathrm{clip}\left(Z_t^{(k)} + \omega_Z \left(\Delta \mathrm{Persist}_t^{(k)}\right)\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-b37f-d389534d3cf7" class="">Where  is the empirical association between driver  and survival of artifacts/agents.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a5-a2d2-f67cbdee7e20"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-9cb3-f1de774d6c50" class="">5) The missing coupling: boundary ↔ records ↔ selection (self/non-self dynamics)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-bf6c-cb50f1a8f719" class="">5.1 Permeability update (how the system opens to non-self)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8077-90f0-ec3586a79651" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pi_{t+1} = \Pi_t + \omega_\Pi\Big( \underbrace{\eta_\Pi G_t}_{\text{growth drives openness}} - \underbrace{\zeta_\Pi \Xi_t}_{\text{noise closes}} - \underbrace{\upsilon_\Pi (1-B_t)}_{\text{weak boundary forces closure}} \Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807c-b266-cbe88fc49428" class="">5.2 Non-self overwrite pressure (the overlooked hidden engine)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-a07f-e8627a864635" class="">Define non-self overwrite term:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-b2dd-f1ec68cbe98a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t^{\text{ns}} = \Pi_t (1-B_t)\cdot \Omega(Z_t,\Sigma_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bf-8fd4-de22ba6884cd" class="">where  increases when selection rewards divergence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b5-9d3d-e2e757c01ba7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Omega(Z_t,\Sigma_t)= c_1(1-\Sigma_t)+c_2(1-Z_t^{\text{truth}})+c_3 Z_t^{\text{coalition}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805a-9b53-e0c71f3c2f2f" class="">Then the total overwrite/noise becomes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b6-a7d3-e581f577ff7a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t \leftarrow \Xi_t + \Xi_t^{\text{ns}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8009-958f-d5497279117a" class="">This closes a key gap: <strong>intangible selection can mechanically increase tangible noise</strong> via permeability + boundary failure.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f3-90fc-dbfab4c06d5a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e9-b2a6-ccf65b44e3cd" class="">6) Selfhood recursion (what “self” actually is in the loop kernel)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804f-b238-d85a50530cc5" class="">Define self as a stack of models  that remain internally consistent under update.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-b8d0-c3cb934f49d6" class="">Add a <strong>self-consistency score</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b6c3-ee1bdf27c339" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t = 1 - \sum_{d=1}^{D_t} w_d \varepsilon_t^{(d)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8020-bc93-d21151a12ce9" class="">A system is “self” (stable) only if:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-ab8e-f6729c20a301" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C_t \ge C_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-85b8-e3480a7fb07d" class="">If , you get “identity break” (your earlier observation: “like a different person”):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808d-b572-dde56363c472" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\text{IdentityBreak}_t \iff C_t&lt;C_{\min}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-88e3-d0580650c4fa" class="">When identity breaks, synchrony and records collapse risk rises:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fa-8823-e518e6d7d2e3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\lambda_{\text{collapse}} \uparrow,\quad \Sigma_t \downarrow</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-a2a8-e64ce9fcef8a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-a5c9-f57b9dc8f55a" class="">7) Cross-species closure (the missing invariants)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809d-9c5a-cf175b3a25c5" class="">Different species instantiate the same kernel with different parameter regimes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803c-b710-c973940422fc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Boundary gain</strong> differs (immune discrimination, sensory resolution).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800f-9c9c-c277ffbe756a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Permeability dynamics</strong> differ (social openness, habitat coupling).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8080-8088-d1bf7e458452" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>Write substrates</strong> differ (genetic/epigenetic vs neural vs cultural).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d8-91e6-c7d15d051e5d" class="">Represent species  with parameter set . Kernel is invariant:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8060-bc78-d3b796f1ced8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
x^{(i)}_{t+1}=F(x^{(i)}_t;\Theta_i)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-bb47-ff43e0b28bf8" class="">Coupling across species occurs through shared environment write-capacity  and shared selection :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c0-bea2-f90136b775fc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U^{env}_{t+1}=U^{env}_t-\gamma\sum_i \Delta R_t^{(i)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-8387-e5838d134b20" class="">This makes “cross-species” non-handwavy: they compete/cooperate over the same write-budget.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808b-b19d-e60c0c08cfa1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b7c1-d402490f4a00" class="">8) What else is still missing (exhaustive list of overlooked gaps)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b7-8000-ea85b02d4a83" class="">These are the remaining “holes” that need explicit variables/equations if you want a closed engine.</p></div><div style="display:contents" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-9258-e72954bba15a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Different agents have different partitions → different , different arrow locally.</li></ul></div><div outputs).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-8c5e-c8a344d18aff" class="">Grand Unified Loop Architecture — <strong>Matrix v1</strong> (deeper, more operator → gates → regime (birth→expansion→dominance→decay)</strong>, spanning <strong>micro→macro, time→space, cross-species</strong>, and including the “intangible” as a <strong>latent selection constraint</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-b609-eeca9e5b2500"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fc-9938-dfb8e5a48b86" class="">0) Core objects</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8023-a3f4-c41dc13b08d7" class="">0.1 State vector (single source of truth)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-ae69-ef1d7ce92bfc" class="">Let the global state at discrete time  be:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" x_t=\begin{bmatrix}
q_t\\
G_t\\
U_t\\

R_t\\

P_t\\

Z_t\\
