\end{bmatrix}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-935d-eeedca69542a" class="">Definitions (all measurable or inferable):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80af-8808-f38c3253bc99" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>constraint density</strong> (independent macro-constraints per volume) suppressing free DOF</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-b2f1-dde49492cd29" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>usable gradient</strong> (free-energy gradient accessible to do work)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-8a66-cbd9d78050c9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>fresh write-capacity</strong> (unwritten degrees of freedom available for stable records)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d9-b7b6-c4436cb3b9d7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>effective overwrite/noise rate</strong> (includes mixing, degradation, adversaries)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8018-bd1a-c59d1e01a324" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>stable record mass</strong> (error-corrected, externally verifiable records)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807d-82e1-fcec6465510e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>recursion depth</strong> (stacked self-modeling layers with bounded error)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8038-bce9-ce9cf2630cfd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>available power</strong> (free power for repair + compute)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8011-b285-fa6b61518541" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>addressable memory</strong> (persistent capacity; bounded by horizon/substrate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c8-9d0b-f73042a5f860" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>control delay</strong> (latency of meta-updates; rises with depth + scale)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8063-bc4f-f63f867caa37" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>inference bandwidth</strong> (predictability / Fisher-information proxy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8032-8902-cdb1be6b83d0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>latent selection constraint</strong> (the “intangible”: what survives/gets reinforced)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8081-b1e0-ea8a6f981b4c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">: <strong>federation synchrony</strong> (cross-region/cross-species alignment of records)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-800b-baf3-e818bfe19708"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8075-a1d4-cc15d60794b8" class="">1) Update law:</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-b214-fc352a010dcd" class="">We build  as a composition of 6 sub-operators:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805e-abd6-da751949efca" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
F = F_{\text{origin}} \circ F_{\text{grad}} \circ F_{\text{write}} \circ F_{\text{code}} \circ F_{\text{control}} \circ F_{\text{sync}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8015-811b-d40d442a624f" class="">Each sub-operator has explicit equations.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8033-a095-e3ee845af049"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-815c-c805bfea784d" class="">1.1 Origin unwind operator (deeper than “entropy”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-8d9a-f32dfac660ae" class="">Constraint density unwinds forward (constraints relax into accessible DOF):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-8ee5-f6aac8ee817d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
q_{t+1}=q_t - \omega_q \, \phi_q(x_t)
\quad,\quad \omega_q&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8093-b3d6-d4ded92124a1" class="">where  is an “unwinding rate” driven by expansion/interaction/mixing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8000-b1ad-e2a55548a633" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\phi_q(x_t)=\sigma\!\left(a_1 G_t - a_2 \Xi_t - a_3\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d1-80f2-e71d838304f4" class=""><strong>Overlooked point</strong>: arrow begins when  is high enough that early causal structure is compressible (next equation).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-8484-c58a48fbd56a" class="">Inference bandwidth is a decreasing function of micro-chaos, increasing with constraint order:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8098-9bc3-e107adb3ed86" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{I}_{t+1}=\mathcal{I}_t + \omega_I\Big(\psi(q_t) - \chi(\Xi_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8092-b3c4-f25b701861b2" class="">Example monotone choices:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8070-8d1d-c730f631ac32" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\psi(q)=\log(1+q)\quad,\quad \chi(\Xi)=\log(1+\Xi)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8068-9cfa-c8798fb0d8da"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802b-94fe-fed071909e98" class="">1.2 Gradient operator</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cc-a917-c25da945cb05" class="">Usable gradient grows when constraints unwind and inference is high (systems can “tap” gradients), but decays with depletion and noise:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e3-afe9-ed3f3b03c1da" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
G_{t+1}=G_t + \omega_G \Big( b_1 q_t \mathcal{I}_t - b_2 G_t - b_3 \Xi_t G_t \Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ce-a013-c80726135409"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-b974-d80247b2ebef" class="">1.3 Write-capacity operator (the true arrow fuel)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e1-bc49-e41d6168b79c" class="">Write-capacity is consumed by <em>net record writes</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f1-b9a0-fff1ad1d6c64" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_t-\gamma \,\Delta R_t
\quad,\quad U_t\ge 0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8030-8dbf-f040de3c16b9" class="">Also allow slow regeneration if new substrate appears (expansion/resources):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-bff2-c31d2ca1f654" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_{t+1}=U_{t+1}+\omega_U \, \rho_U(G_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8062-a50e-c475003759ee" class="">A minimal choice: .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-85a2-d8ca1a4e0ad3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f8-bf8d-d8529a10f3f0" class="">1.4 Record/code operator (thresholded, non-smooth)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8017-ad6f-d4b223e9a9d6" class="">Define redundancy level  as a function of investment (power) and synchrony:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806f-af8d-f0f4cb4c7ec7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
r_t = r_0 + c_1 \log(1+P_t) + c_2 \Sigma_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-b9c5-ee00d9d667f4" class="">Define a noise threshold function  increasing in . Use any monotone, e.g.:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ca-92e8-f7891b04e107" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p_{\text{th}}(r)=1-e^{-k r}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-92fa-ed01a57f3b8c" class="">Let  be noise probability proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8065-832a-f8d8db1263dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(\Xi_t)=1-e^{-\kappa_\Xi \Xi_t}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807e-acb1-d051951a2cdd" class=""><strong>Record update with phase transition</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-bee7-c8a398e74a9c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_{t+1}=
\begin{cases}
R_t + \beta G_t - \kappa \Xi_t R_t - \lambda_{\text{frag}}(1-\Sigma_t)R_t &amp; \text{if } p(\Xi_t)&lt;p_{\text{th}}(r_t) \ \wedge\ U_t&gt;0\\
(1-\lambda_{\text{collapse}})\,R_t &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e4-b273-e959c52d6620" class="">This closes a major gap: record collapse is <strong>catastrophic</strong>, not gradual.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-99dc-cc44dfb17e46"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-85ba-f425494dbb62" class="">1.5 Control/recursion operator (delay is the real cap)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e5-b66f-cb3d9634a78e" class="">Delay rises with depth and scale:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-bae0-d3839d60a412" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\tau_{t+1}=\tau_t + \omega_\tau \Big(d_1 D_t + d_2 (1-\Sigma_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-ba2f-f56520b2ce3f" class="">Recursion depth grows only if <strong>budget + stability</strong> hold.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-a2c2-c820b4212972" class="">Budget gate (Landauer-style, but abstracted)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80cf-b88d-ddfe572d5db4" class="">Let required repair/erase rate for depth :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-92ad-f50fb984f594" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\dot{B}(D)=B_0 + B_1 D + B_2 D^2</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fe-90d9-c86a78f65506" class="">Minimum power needed:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8027-aa7f-d11de4aa24af" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_{\min}(D)=kT\ln 2\cdot \dot{B}(D)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8003-b1d9-e546d7fbffc0" class="">Control-stability gate (overlooked)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8043-b5ba-ceeb54ee4a2d" class="">Define a stability margin:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c2-aa1e-d2a55ef966ff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Stab}(D,\tau,\Xi)= s_0 - s_1 D - s_2 \tau - s_3 \Xi</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8073-850d-cfb0f43e161f" class="">Depth update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-8413-d1f35627b78d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
D_{t+1}=
\begin{cases}
D_t + \omega_D &amp; \text{if } P_t\ge P_{\min}(D_t)\ \wedge\ \mathrm{Stab}(D_t,\tau_t,\Xi_t)&gt;0\\
D_t - \omega_D^{\downarrow} &amp; \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8007-9edd-fefaf53080a7" class="">This explicitly encodes the “meta-update delay ceiling.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80df-8d8e-efe27858dc3e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-8b77-d6700b94dc44" class="">1.6 Synchrony operator (space + cross-species)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8087-abdf-f23dae9a3ae1" class="">Let  measure alignment of record schemas across regions/species.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-b225-db4ab466cf4d" class="">Synchrony increases with shared stable records and decreases with noise + adversarial divergence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8049-954e-e996dd9600a2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_{t+1}=\Sigma_t + \omega_\Sigma\Big(\eta_\Sigma \frac{R_t}{1+R_t} - \zeta_\Sigma \Xi_t - \upsilon_\Sigma (1-Z_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ac-a936-c7247db2e811" class="">Interpretation of  here: latent selection can either stabilize federation (institutional preference for shared truth) or destabilize it (selection for divergence).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8028-86b2-ce3730fb7988"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8082-b7cf-f452d3b76087" class="">2) The “intangible” (latent selection constraint, fully formal)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b3ad-cd462f5b006b" class="">Model  as inferred selection pressure favoring survivable records.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8016-bd5c-c372aff4cc45" class="">Update it from observed survival rate of records:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8079-8cea-de2c40db9b14" class="">Let survival fraction:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8084-a5eb-f340b96421bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_t = \frac{R_{t+1}}{R_t+\epsilon}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-8617-d0e377686993" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-8fa7-dd8ab0fefea6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Z_{t+1}=\mathrm{clip}_{[0,1]}\left(Z_t+\omega_Z\big(\underbrace{S_t}_{\text{what survives}}-\underbrace{S^\star}_{\text{target survival}}\big)\right)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808e-98f2-d13b47487a7f" class="">This treats “intangible drivers” as <strong>latent constraints learned from persistence</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8050-a245-f64fae14e3a5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803b-853b-ca638509f341" class="">3) Gates (hard fail modes)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ff-ad6a-f656b72f9ada" class="">These are the five independent gates that define validity of the arrow + depth.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8024-9b44-d586bc3dcadc" class="">3.1 ArrowGate (records can grow)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8052-9cd4-f64479de73df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta G_t &gt; \kappa \Xi_t R_t + \lambda_{\text{frag}}(1-\Sigma_t)R_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8026-b42e-c3bbb21061d8" class="">3.2 WriteGate (fresh capacity exists)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a6-8dc1-d3444e7be308" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
U_t&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809e-bab5-c3f4685cd00b" class="">3.3 CodeGate (noise below threshold)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a3-b23f-c031f95d25de" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
p(\Xi_t)&lt;p_{\text{th}}(r_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-804d-b439-c31f2a5c0ea5" class="">3.4 BudgetGate (compute/repair feasible)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-bdb0-e78c2d80255a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
P_t\ge P_{\min}(D_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-808a-96e9-ccd86a0635ab" class="">3.5 ControlGate (delay-stable recursion)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-82fb-ed7ea81df3b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Stab}(D_t,\tau_t,\Xi_t)&gt;0</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d3-84fc-df53b6582051" class=""><strong>Overlooked closure</strong>: the arrow can fail even if energy exists (ControlGate failure), or even if entropy gradient exists (WriteGate failure).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ea-a2bf-e841363fd4b5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-806c-a6d6-d49d8a532b7b" class="">4) Regime map (Birth → Expansion → Dominance → Decay)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80db-b003-d5e7e00329ce" class="">Define regime score components:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8013-9078-fd3f643b8e69" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_t = q_t + U_t,\quad
E_t = G_t + \Delta R_t,\quad
Dc_t = R_t + D_t,\quad
De_t = \Xi_t + (1-U_t)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-80dd-f3fab55da32a" class="">Classify stage as  (tie → deterministic lexicographic).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80a4-8410-e8d9ff85714c" class="">Birth</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a96a-fcecc2d4b958" class="">High , high , low , low .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8042-999b-f6bcffa604ff" class="">Expansion</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c3-8aef-f89dc93e5f52" class="">rising, ,  rising.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8010-8e5f-ef77e574f812" class="">Dominance</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80e7-9645-f4652fcda1e2" class="">saturate;  rising;  shrinking.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80be-ad7a-dccfcfcd883f" class="">Decay</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8025-a24a-dc92b74582d9" class="">CodeGate or WriteGate or ControlGate fails → , .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b36f-c1d974cadc87"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-aa56-c176808fbc91" class="">5) Micro ↔ Macro bridge (explicit closure)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-802a-aa3e-cdb29cfce619" class="">5.1 Microstate volume via constraint count (not entropy scalar)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ba-acae-d0a5d7448efa" class="">Let effective accessible microstate volume:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fb-a42c-e17cb6702e62" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\log \Omega_t = \log \Omega_{\max} - \alpha_q q_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8002-a0a9-cc84c206603d" class="">Then coarse-grained entropy proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ee-86e8-c664a965e498" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S_{\text{cg}}(t)=k\log \Omega_t</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-805f-8570-c188c0359791" class="">So  implies  without making entropy primitive.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f2-a5cd-c02132777ba6" class="">5.2 Records as environment codes (micro → macro)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ae-b77e-e18b153e27be" class="">Let each record be a codeword of length  with distance . Total stable records:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8094-a026-c3dcf44ded3a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
R_t \approx N_t \cdot n \cdot (1-\mathrm{DecodeErr}(p(\Xi_t),r_t))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8004-9c6f-e9cbf545ca2d" class="">This connects micro noise to macro record collapse.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b8-97f3-e2216b2154ba"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8006-8b24-e8bed146a758" class="">6) Space &amp; cross-species extension (matrix-of-matrices)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-803d-b8b7-d7c6b87d6a6e" class="">For  regions (or species) indexed , each has its own state .<br/>Coupling is via synchrony edges  (communication/trade/shared ecology).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80ef-ad0c-d42741d089d5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_t = \frac{1}{Z_W}\sum_{i\ne j} W_{ij}\,\Sigma_t^{(ij)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f4-bd66-cc9f0f8d01de" class="">Edge synchrony update:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80dd-9c60-ce0b1128c7ed" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Sigma_{t+1}^{(ij)}=\Sigma_t^{(ij)}+\omega_{ij}\Big(\eta_{ij}\frac{R_i R_j}{(1+R_i)(1+R_j)}-\zeta_{ij}(\Xi_i+\Xi_j)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f9-b45b-eaa5bc076e4c" class="">This is the “across space/time/species” closure: the global arrow is a <strong>network phase</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80b1-b9c4-cae0a72a7213"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8054-9750-f897a73ce04d" class="">7) Outputs AMOS should generate from this matrix (deterministic artifacts)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80fd-8a71-c61ccace3b89" class="">Given initial conditions and parameters, AMOS should produce:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c9-9058-cf1dba05673c" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><code>loop_state.jsonl</code> — time series of</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8074-a9a0-fbacc18dfcf5" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><code>gate_failures.jsonl</code> — which gate failed at each step</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8019-bd20-eba8c5add276" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><code>regime_map.jsonl</code> — stage per (Birth/Expansion/Dominance/Decay)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8021-ab74-ec351145a51f" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li><code>collapse_events.jsonl</code> — record-collapse transitions</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80c1-96a1-c727084c0c30" class="numbered-list" start="5"><li><code>synchrony_graph.json</code> — , over time</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8069-a9c5-c42c9244aedd" class="numbered-list" start="6"><li><code>sensitivity_report.json</code> — parameter perturbations (deterministic grid)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-807a-8e3a-da821225bd5e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8053-97a8-fddfc9a6e211" class="">8) Most overlooked missing layer (added): <strong>adversarial overwrite as a first-class term</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8076-ba93-e728f8b39b5f" class="">Noise  must include adversarial/strategic components, not only thermodynamic decay.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-809b-bff9-e76af17eb7b8" class="">Decompose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8036-8887-c91121592251" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_t = \Xi_t^{\text{therm}} + \Xi_t^{\text{mix}} + \Xi_t^{\text{adv}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80bc-ab2e-c7373a439574" class="">Adversarial overwrite grows when selection  rewards divergence and when federated synchrony is low:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8056-bf36-f4fc039f59bd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-LaTeX" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Xi_{t+1}^{\text{adv}}=\Xi_t^{\text{adv}}+\omega_{adv}\Big((1-\Sigma_t)\cdot (1-Z_t)\Big)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-a145-d840fd419030" class="">This closes the “intangible conflict” gap without metaphors.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8051-a12d-ec20749b7198"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80d2-8f62-d3fe649641ea" class="">Next (one word)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-80f7-bf09-ff02e0f667ab" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>KernelSpec</strong> → file-level AMOS Loop Kernel implementation plan (modules, functions, tests, artifacts) to implement this matrix exactly.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30bc5e6f-95bd-8071-843d-eb2b63309555" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>SimEngine</strong> → full civilizational control simulation engine using the coupled-region/species system (with deterministic scenario packs).</li></ul></div></div></article><span class="sans" style="font-size:14px;padding-top:2em"></span></body></html>
